> DEVELOPER

Lane B: Receive 数据展示页 + 可视化组件

  ## 背景
  receive feature 的 service 层已 100% 完成（帧解析、数据缓冲、字段值查询全部就位），
  display feature 也已实现星座图（projectScatter）和波形图（projectChartSeries）的数据投影层。
  但缺少：1) 接收数据展示页面 2) ECharts 渲染组件。

  ## 直接合同（按顺序读取）
  1. codestable/features/rewrite-ui-architecture/rewrite-ui-architecture-design.md — 查找 receive/display 页面的设计规格
  2. rewrite/src/features/receive/core/index.ts — ReceiveService public API
  3. rewrite/src/features/receive/core/types.ts — receive 相关类型
  4. rewrite/src/features/display/ — display feature 全部文件（含 scatter/chart projection）
  5. rewrite/src/features/receive/ — receive feature 全部文件
  6. codestable/quality/rewrite-frontend-conventions.md — 前端规范
  7. rewrite/src/pages/ — 参考已有页面的实现模式

  ## 任务

  ### 第一步：摸底
  - 读取 receive service 和 display service 的全部 public API
  - 确认 receive 页面在 UI 架构设计文档中的定位（如果有设计则按设计走，没有则 brainstorm 后暂停）
  - 确认 display 的 scatter/chart projection 输出的数据格式

  ### 第二步：实现接收数据页面
  如果设计文档中有 receive 页面设计，按设计实现。如果没有，先产出 brainstorm 文档暂停等确认。

  页面应至少包含：
  - 接收帧统计（帧匹配率、错误数、数据源状态）
  - 接收数据表格（帧名/字段值/时间戳）
  - 实时数据流指示

  ### 第三步：实现可视化组件
  基于 display feature 的 projectScatter/projectChartSeries 输出：
  - 星座图组件（ECharts scatter，I/Q 信号展示）
  - 波形图组件（ECharts line，时间序列展示）

  注意：
  - 检查项目是否已安装 echarts 和 vue-echarts，没有则安装
  - 遵循前端 conventions 中的组件规范
  - 参考旧系统 src/components/ 下的可视化实现作为行为参考（不抄代码）

  ### 第四步：集成到 receive 页面或独立页面
  根据 UI 架构设计文档决定可视化放在 receive 页面内还是独立页面。

  ## 验证
  1. pnpm -C rewrite build
  2. pnpm -C rewrite lint
  3. pnpm -C rewrite test --run
  4. 启动 dev 确认页面渲染正常

  ## 完成后提交

> AGENT

1	# 全局 UI 架构 Design
2	
3	## 元数据
4	
5	- **类型**: design（结论和变化）
6	- **直接合同**: rewrite-frontend-conventions.md, rewrite-frontend-checklist.md, 本文档
7	- **边界护栏**: CLAUDE.md, rewrite-target-structure.md, rewrite-display-ui-design.md
8	- **日期**: 2026-05-12
9	- **brainstorm**: rewrite-ui-architecture-brainstorm.md
10	- **状态**: 已锁定（用户已确认全部 open questions）
11	
12	---
13	
14	## D1 导航体系
15	
16	**方案**: 二级折叠侧边栏 + 顶部连接选择器 + 面包屑
17	
18	```
19	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
20	│ QHeader [48px]                                                  │
21	│ [☰] 东方红上位机  [连接选择器] [状态指示] [暗色切换] [窗口控制]  │
22	├──────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
23	│ QDrawer  │ QPageContainer                                       │
24	│ [232px]  │                                                      │
25	│          │  ┌─ QBreadcrumbs (仅二级页面) ──────────────────┐   │
26	│ 运行总览 │  │                                              │   │
27	│ 连接管理 │  │  页面内容                                      │   │
28	│ ▼ 帧管理 │  │                                              │   │
29	│   帧列表 │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │
30	│   帧编辑 │                                                      │
31	│ ▼ 数据交互│                                                      │
32	│   发送   │                                                      │
33	│   接收展示│                                                      │
34	│ 接收配置 │                                                      │
35	│ 历史分析 │                                                      │
36	│ SCOE配置 │                                                      │
37	│ 高速存储 │                                                      │
38	│ 系统设置 │                                                      │
39	└──────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
40	```
41	
42	**实现要点**:
43	- QDrawer: show-if-above, :width="232", :mini="miniState", bordered
44	- 一级菜单: QItem + :to 路由绑定
45	- 二级折叠: QExpansionItem（帧管理→帧列表+帧编辑，数据交互→发送+接收展示）
46	- 连接选择器: QSelect下拉，数据源 connection.selectTransportTargets()
47	- 面包屑: QBreadcrumbs，从路由meta生成
48	- mini状态: localStorage持久化，hover临时展开
49	
50	**MVP入口**（对应旧系统10个入口）:
51	| 路由 | 页面 | Feature |
52	|------|------|---------|
53	| / | 运行总览 | status |
54	| /connection | 连接管理 | connection |
55	| /frames | 帧列表 | frame |
56	| /frames/editor/:frameId? | 帧编辑 | frame |
57	| /frames/send | 帧发送 | send+task |
58	| /display | 实时展示 | display+receive |
59	| /receive/config | 接收配置 | receive+display |
60	| /history | 历史分析 | storage+display |
61	| /command-ingress | SCOE配置 | command-ingress |
62	| /storage | 高速存储 | storage |
63	| /settings | 系统设置 | settings |
64	
65	---
66	
67	## D2 布局骨架
68	
69	3种标准布局模式，作为设计指南。各页面用CSS grid/flex + token实现。
70	
71	### 模式A: 主从双栏
72	
73	```
74	┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
75	│  页面标题 + 操作按钮                                 │
76	├──────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┤
77	│  主区域(flex:1)       │  侧边面板(固定宽度)          │
78	│  - 表格/列表          │  - 详情/配置                 │
79	│  - overflow: auto     │  - 280px~360px              │
80	└──────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┘
81	```
82	适用: 连接管理、帧列表、接收配置、历史分析(可选QSplitter)
83	
84	### 模式B: 三栏固定+弹性
85	
86	```
87	┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
88	│  页面标题 + 操作按钮                                       │
89	├──────────┬───────────────────────┬───────────────────────┤
90	│  左栏     │  中栏(flex:1)          │  右栏                 │
91	│  240px    │  - 实例/编辑区         │  300px                │
92	│  - 格式选择│  - 主工作区            │  - 预览/结果          │
93	└──────────┴───────────────────────┴───────────────────────┘
94	```
95	适用: 帧发送
96	
97	### 模式C: 单栏居中
98	
99	```
100	┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
101	│  页面标题 + 操作按钮                                 │
102	├────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
103	│        ┌──────────────────────────────┐             │
104	│        │  主内容区(max-width: 1120px)  │             │
105	│        │  margin: 0 auto              │             │
106	│        └──────────────────────────────┘             │
107	└────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
108	```
109	适用: 高速存储、系统设置、SCOE配置
110	
111	---
112	
113	## D3 共享 Widget
114	
115	| Widget | 用途 | 接口核心 | 优先级 |
116	|--------|------|---------|--------|
117	| **DataTable** | 通用数据表格 | rows, columns, rowKey, virtualScroll, selection, loading, serverMode | P0 |
118	| **StatusBadge** | 状态标签 | status(enum), size, outlined | P1 |
119	| **TableToolbar** | 表格工具栏 | searchable, filterable, actions[], selectedCount | P1 |
120	| **SendTargetSelector** | 发送目标选择 | modelValue(targetId), connectionType | P1 |
121	| **FrameSelector** | 帧选择下拉 | modelValue(frameId), direction, searchable | P1 |
122	| **ChartWidget** | 图表容器 | type, data, options, loading | P2 |
123	
124	**DataTable 关键能力**:
125	- client模式: 全量数据 + virtual-scroll（行数≤1000）
126	- server模式: QTable @request + feature service分页/排序（行数>1000）
127	- 空状态 #no-data slot, loading prop, selection prop
128	- row-key 必须稳定唯一标识符
129	
130	**不提前建**: FormBuilder, ConfirmDialog(用$q.dialog()), FormDialog, ActionButtonGroup
131	**归feature**: ParameterTable→display, ConnectionConfigForm→connection, FieldEditor→frame
132	
133	---
134	
135	## D4 实时数据展示架构
136	
137	```
138	receive service
139	    │ 16ms routingTick
140	    ▼
141	DisplayService.ingestSourceMaterial()
142	    │ 写入 buffer (非响应式)
143	    ▼
144	DisplayService.recomputeSnapshot()
145	    │ 计算 projection → 更新 state
146	    ▼
147	DisplaySelectors (只读快照)
148	    │ selectTable1Rows(), selectChartSeries()
149	    ▼
150	useDisplayRefresh (features/display/composables/)
151	    │ rAF + cadenceMs(默认200ms)
152	    │ pendingChanges 标记位
153	    │ shallowRef 整组替换
154	    ▼
155	Vue 组件 (props 驱动)
156	    │ DataTable + ChartWidget + ScatterWidget
157	    ▼
158	DOM (Quasar + ECharts)
159	```
160	
161	**关键参数**:
162	| 场景 | cadenceMs | 实现方式 |
163	|------|-----------|---------|
164	| 默认 | 200ms | rAF + cadence 节流 |
165	| 快速过程 | 100ms | cadenceMs: 100 |
166	| 慢速过程 | 1000ms | cadenceMs: 1000 |
167	| 后台/最小化 | 暂停 | document.hidden 检测 |
168	
169	**视觉反馈**:
170	- 值变化: CSS @keyframes value-flash 600ms
171	- 告警: StatusBadge critical→negative+闪烁, warning→warning+背景色
172	- 数据过期: opacity:0.6 + grayscale + 时间戳
173	- 后台恢复: 补偿更新
174	
175	---
176	
177	## D5 暗色模式策略
178	
179	**决策**: 只做暗色（默认唯一主题），不实施双主题切换
180	
181	**实施**:
182	- 将现有 --rw-color-surface-* 值改为暗色值（深灰/深蓝灰背景）
183	- 将 --rw-color-text-* 值改为浅色值
184	- Quasar brand 色保持不变
185	- 图表6色保持不变（暗色背景下对比度充足）
186	- 不引入 body--dark 双层结构
187	
188	**后续**: 如需亮色模式，需全局重构token为 :root + .body--dark 双层结构
189	
190	---
191	
192	## D6 路由表
193	
194	```typescript
195	// routes.ts
196	const routes = [
197	  {
198	    path: '/',
199	    component: () => import('app/AppShell.vue'),
200	    children: [
201	      { path: '', name: 'home', component: () => import('pages/HomePage.vue') },

208	          { path: 'send', name: 'frame-send', component: () => import('pages/SendPage.vue') },
209	        ],
210	      },
211	      { path: 'display', name: 'display', component: () => import('pages/DisplayPage.vue') },
212	      { path: 'receive/config', name: 'receive-config', component: () => import('pages/ReceiveConfigPage.vue') },
213	      { path: 'history', name: 'history', component: () => import('pages/HistoryPage.vue') },
214	      { path: 'command-ingress', name: 'command-ingress', component: () => import('pages/CommandIngressPage.vue') },
215	      { path: 'storage', name: 'storage', component: () => import('pages/StoragePage.vue') },
216	      { path: 'settings', name: 'settings', component: () => import('pages/SettingsPage.vue') },
217	    ],
218	  },
219	];
220	```
221	
222	---
223	
224	## 标识对齐
225	
226	路由参数与feature ID类型对齐:
227	- /frames/editor/:frameId → FrameAsset.frameId
228	- 未来 /tasks/:taskId → TaskDefinition.taskId（需调整task types）

1	import type {
2	  ChartDisplayPreference,
3	  ChartPoint,
4	  ChartSeriesProjection,
5	  DisplayFieldMaterial,
6	  DisplayPreferences,
7	  DisplayProjection,
8	  ScatterDisplayPreference,
9	  ScatterProjection,
10	  ScatterSourceBinding,
11	  TableRowProjection,
12	} from './types';
13	
14	export function projectTableRows(
15	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
16	  selectedGroupId: string,
17	  selectedItems: readonly string[],
18	): TableRowProjection[] {
19	  const filtered = selectedGroupId
20	    ? fields.filter((f) => f.groupId === selectedGroupId)
21	    : fields;
22	
23	  if (selectedItems.length === 0) {
24	    return filtered.map(toRow);
25	  }
26	
27	  const itemSet = new Set(selectedItems);
28	  return filtered.filter((f) => itemSet.has(f.dataItemId)).map(toRow);
29	}
30	
31	function toRow(f: DisplayFieldMaterial): TableRowProjection {
32	  return {
33	    groupId: f.groupId,
34	    dataItemId: f.dataItemId,
35	    fieldName: f.fieldName,
36	    value: f.value,
37	    displayValue: f.displayValue,
38	    ...(f.updatedAt ? { updatedAt: f.updatedAt } : {}),
39	  };
40	}
41	
42	export function projectChartSeries(
43	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
44	  preference: ChartDisplayPreference,
45	  historyBuffer: ReadonlyMap<string, ChartPoint[]>,
46	): ChartSeriesProjection[] {
47	  const selectedItems = preference.selectedItems;
48	
49	  if (selectedItems.length === 0) {
50	    return [];
51	  }
52	
53	  const maxPoints = preference.performance.maxPoints;
54	  const result: ChartSeriesProjection[] = [];
55	
56	  for (const fieldId of selectedItems) {
57	    const field = fields.find((f) => `${f.groupId}:${f.dataItemId}` === fieldId);
58	    const history = historyBuffer.get(fieldId);
59	    const points: ChartPoint[] = history ? history.slice(-maxPoints) : [];
60	
61	    result.push({
62	      fieldId,
63	      fieldName: field?.fieldName ?? fieldId,
64	      points,
65	    });
66	  }
67	
68	  return result;
69	}
70	
71	export function projectScatter(
72	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
73	  preference: ScatterDisplayPreference,
74	): ScatterProjection {
75	  const iField = findFieldByBinding(fields, preference.iSource);
76	  const qField = findFieldByBinding(fields, preference.qSource);
77	
78	  if (!iField || !qField) {
79	    return { points: [], sampleCount: 0 };
80	  }
81	
82	  const iVal = typeof iField.value === 'number' ? iField.value : null;
83	  const qVal = typeof qField.value === 'number' ? qField.value : null;
84	
85	  if (iVal === null || qVal === null) {
86	    return { points: [], sampleCount: 0 };
87	  }
88	
89	  return {
90	    points: [{ i: iVal, q: qVal }],
91	    sampleCount: 1,
92	  };
93	}
94	
95	function findFieldByBinding(
96	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
97	  binding: ScatterSourceBinding,
98	): DisplayFieldMaterial | undefined {
99	  if (!binding.groupId || !binding.dataItemId) {
100	    return undefined;
101	  }
102	  return fields.find(
103	    (f) => f.groupId === binding.groupId && f.dataItemId === binding.dataItemId,
104	  );
105	}
106	
107	export function computeDisplayProjection(
108	  fields: readonly DisplayFieldMaterial[],
109	  preferences: DisplayPreferences,
110	  historyBuffer: ReadonlyMap<string, ChartPoint[]>,
111	): DisplayProjection {
112	  return {
113	    table1Rows: projectTableRows(fields, preferences.table1.selectedGroupId, preferences.table1.selectedItems),
114	    table2Rows: projectTableRows(fields, preferences.table2.selectedGroupId, preferences.table2.selectedItems),
115	    chartSeries: projectChartSeries(fields, preferences.chart, historyBuffer),
116	    scatter: projectScatter(fields, preferences.scatter),
117	  };
118	}
119	
```

11	export function selectDisplaySnapshot(source: ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot) {

```
1	import {
2	  cloneDisplaySnapshot,
3	  createDefaultDisplaySnapshot,
4	  computeDisplayProjection,
5	  applyDisplayPreferencesPatch,
6	  type ChartPoint,
7	  type DisplayFieldMaterial,
8	  type DisplayPreferences,
9	  type DisplayPreferencesPatch,
10	  type DisplaySourceAvailability,
11	  type DisplaySourceMaterial,
12	  type DisplaySnapshot,
13	  type DisplayValidationIssue,
14	  type ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot,
15	  type ReadonlyDisplayPreferences,
16	  type TableRowProjection,
17	  type ChartSeriesProjection,
18	  type ScatterProjection,
19	} from '../core';
20	import {
21	  selectAvailability,
22	  selectChartSeries,
23	  selectDisplaySnapshot,
24	  selectPreferences,
25	  selectScatterProjection,
26	  selectTable1Rows,
27	  selectTable2Rows,
28	} from '../selectors';
29	import { createDisplayState, type DisplayStateContainer } from '../state';
30	
31	export interface DisplayReader {
32	  getSnapshot(): ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot;
33	  getPreferences(): ReadonlyDisplayPreferences;
34	  getTable1Rows(): TableRowProjection[];
35	  getTable2Rows(): TableRowProjection[];
36	  getChartSeries(): ChartSeriesProjection[];
37	  getScatterProjection(): ScatterProjection;
38	  getAvailability(): DisplaySourceAvailability;
39	}
40	
41	export interface DisplayOperationResult {
42	  readonly ok: boolean;
43	  readonly issues: readonly DisplayValidationIssue[];
44	  readonly snapshot: ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot;
45	}
46	
47	export interface DisplayService extends DisplayReader {
48	  updatePreferences(patch: DisplayPreferencesPatch): DisplayOperationResult;
49	  ingestSourceMaterial(material: DisplaySourceMaterial): DisplayOperationResult;
50	  clearProjection(): DisplayOperationResult;
51	  reset(): DisplayOperationResult;
52	}
53	
54	interface DisplayBuffer {
55	  chartHistory: Map<string, ChartPoint[]>;
56	  sourceFields: DisplayFieldMaterial[];
57	}
58	
59	function recomputeSnapshot(
60	  buffer: DisplayBuffer,
61	  preferences: DisplayPreferences,
62	  availability: DisplaySourceAvailability,
63	  state: DisplayStateContainer,
64	): DisplaySnapshot {
65	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
66	  const projection = computeDisplayProjection(
67	    buffer.sourceFields,
68	    preferences,
69	    buffer.chartHistory,
70	  );
71	
72	  return state.replaceSnapshot({
73	    schemaVersion: state.getSnapshot().schemaVersion,
74	    preferences,
75	    projection,
76	    availability,
77	    lastUpdatedAt: now,
78	  });
79	}
80	
81	function toOperationResult(
82	  snapshot: DisplaySnapshot,
83	  issues: readonly DisplayValidationIssue[],
84	): DisplayOperationResult {
85	  return {
86	    ok: issues.every((i) => i.severity !== 'error'),
87	    issues,
88	    snapshot: cloneDisplaySnapshot(snapshot),
89	  };
90	}
91	
92	export function createDisplayReader(
93	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot,
94	): DisplayReader {
95	  return {
96	    getSnapshot() {
97	      return selectDisplaySnapshot(snapshotProvider());
98	    },
99	    getPreferences() {
100	      return selectPreferences(snapshotProvider());
101	    },
102	    getTable1Rows() {
103	      return selectTable1Rows(snapshotProvider());
104	    },
105	    getTable2Rows() {
106	      return selectTable2Rows(snapshotProvider());
107	    },
108	    getChartSeries() {
109	      return selectChartSeries(snapshotProvider());
110	    },
111	    getScatterProjection() {
112	      return selectScatterProjection(snapshotProvider());
113	    },
114	    getAvailability() {
115	      return selectAvailability(snapshotProvider());
116	    },
117	  };
118	}
119	
120	export function createDisplayService(
121	  state: DisplayStateContainer = createDisplayState(),
122	): DisplayService {
123	  const reader = createDisplayReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
124	
125	  const buffer: DisplayBuffer = {
126	    chartHistory: new Map(),
127	    sourceFields: [],
128	  };
129	
130	  return {
131	    ...reader,
132	
133	    updatePreferences(patch) {
134	      const current = state.getSnapshot();
135	      const result = applyDisplayPreferencesPatch(current, patch);
136	      const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
137	        buffer,
138	        result.snapshot.preferences,
139	        current.availability,
140	        state,
141	      );
142	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, result.issues);
143	    },
144	
145	    ingestSourceMaterial(material) {
146	      const current = state.getSnapshot();
147	
148	      if (material.fields) {
149	        buffer.sourceFields = material.fields.map((f) => ({ ...f }));
150	      }
151	
152	      if (material.availability) {
153	        const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
154	          buffer,
155	          current.preferences,
156	          material.availability,
157	          state,
158	        );
159	        return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
160	      }
161	
162	      const snapshot = recomputeSnapshot(
163	        buffer,
164	        current.preferences,
165	        current.availability,
166	        state,
167	      );
168	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
169	    },
170	
171	    clearProjection() {
172	      const current = state.getSnapshot();
173	      buffer.sourceFields = [];
174	      buffer.chartHistory.clear();
175	
176	      const snapshot = state.replaceSnapshot({
177	        ...createDefaultDisplaySnapshot(),
178	        preferences: current.preferences,
179	        availability: current.availability,
180	      });
181	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
182	    },
183	
184	    reset() {
185	      buffer.sourceFields = [];
186	      buffer.chartHistory.clear();
187	
188	      const snapshot = state.resetSnapshot();
189	      return toOperationResult(snapshot, []);
190	    },
191	  };
192	}
193	
```

6	} from '@/features/frame';
7	import {
8	  createReceiveInputErrorOutcome,
9	  createStaleReceiveInputOutcome,
10	  processReceiveBatch,
11	  type ReceiveBatchOutcome,
12	  type ReceiveEventSnapshot,
13	  type ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot,
14	  type ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot,
15	  type ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot,
16	  type ReceiveInputBatch,
17	  type ReceiveInputError,
18	  type ReceiveIssue,
19	  type ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot,
20	  type ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot,
21	  type ReceiveStateSnapshot,
22	  type ReceiveUiSnapshot,
23	} from '../core';
24	import {
25	  cloneBatchOutcome,
26	  cloneFrameReferences,
27	  cloneIssue,
28	  cloneReferenceSnapshot,
29	  cloneStateSnapshot,
30	} from '../core/clone';
31	import {
32	  selectReceiveCounters,
33	  selectReceiveEvents,
34	  selectReceiveFieldValues,
35	  selectReceiveFrameStats,
36	  selectReceiveRecentInputs,
37	  selectReceiveSnapshot,
38	  selectReceiveSourceStats,
39	  selectReceiveUiSnapshot,
40	  type ReceiveFieldValueQuery,
41	  type ReceiveFrameStatsQuery,
42	  type ReceiveRecentInputQuery,
43	} from '../selectors';
44	import {
45	  createReceiveState,
46	  type ReceiveResetScope,
47	  type ReceiveStateContainer,
48	} from '../state';
49	
50	export type ReceiveInputEvent =
51	  | {
52	      readonly kind: 'batch';
53	      readonly batch: ReceiveInputBatch;
54	    }
55	  | {
56	      readonly kind: 'error';
57	      readonly error: ReceiveInputError;
58	    };
59	
60	export interface ReceiveInputSource {
61	  drainEvents(): Promise<readonly ReceiveInputEvent[]>;
62	}
63	
64	export interface ReceiveReader {
65	  getSnapshot(): ReceiveStateSnapshot;
66	  getUiSnapshot(): ReceiveUiSnapshot;
67	  getCounters(): ReturnType<typeof selectReceiveCounters>;
68	  listFrameStats(query?: ReceiveFrameStatsQuery): ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot[];
69	  listSourceStats(): ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot[];
70	  listFieldValues(query?: ReceiveFieldValueQuery): ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot[];
71	  listRecentInputs(query?: ReceiveRecentInputQuery): ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot[];
72	  listEvents(): ReceiveEventSnapshot[];
73	}
74	
75	export interface ReceiveService extends ReceiveReader {
76	  refreshFrameReferences(frames?: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[]): ReceiveServiceOutcome;
77	  ingestBatch(batch: ReceiveInputBatch): ReceiveServiceOutcome;
78	  recordInputError(error: ReceiveInputError): ReceiveServiceOutcome;
79	  drainInputSource(source: ReceiveInputSource): Promise<ReceiveServiceOutcome>;
80	  reset(scope?: ReceiveResetScope): ReceiveServiceOutcome;
81	}
82	
83	export interface ReceiveServiceOutcome {
84	  readonly ok: boolean;
85	  readonly outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[];
86	  readonly snapshot: ReceiveStateSnapshot;
87	  readonly issues: readonly ReceiveIssue[];
88	  readonly reference?: ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot;
89	}
90	
91	export interface CreateReceiveServiceOptions {
92	  readonly frameReader?: Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'findFrames'>;
93	  readonly state?: ReceiveStateContainer;
94	  readonly now?: () => string;
95	}
96	
97	
98	const INITIAL_REFERENCE_REFRESHED_AT = '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';
99	
100	function createDefaultFrameReader(): FrameAssetReader {
101	  return createFrameAssetReader(() => ({
102	    frames: [],
103	  }));
104	}
105	
106	function outcomeIsOk(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): boolean {
107	  return outcome.kind === 'matched' || outcome.kind === 'unmatched';
108	}
109	
110	function serviceOutcome(
111	  snapshot: ReceiveStateSnapshot,
112	  outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[] = [],
113	  reference?: ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot,
114	): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
115	  const issues = outcomes.flatMap((item) => item.issues.map(cloneIssue));
116	  return {
117	    ok: outcomes.every(outcomeIsOk),
118	    outcomes: outcomes.map(cloneBatchOutcome),
119	    snapshot: cloneStateSnapshot(snapshot),
120	    issues,
121	    ...(reference ? { reference: cloneReferenceSnapshot(reference) } : {}),
122	  };
123	}
124	
125	export function createReceiveReader(
126	  snapshotProvider: () => ReceiveStateSnapshot,
127	): ReceiveReader {
128	  return {
129	    getSnapshot() {
130	      return selectReceiveSnapshot(snapshotProvider());
131	    },
132	
133	    getUiSnapshot() {
134	      return selectReceiveUiSnapshot(snapshotProvider());
135	    },
136	
137	    getCounters() {
138	      return selectReceiveCounters(snapshotProvider());
139	    },
140	
141	    listFrameStats(query) {
142	      return selectReceiveFrameStats(snapshotProvider(), query);
143	    },
144	
145	    listSourceStats() {
146	      return selectReceiveSourceStats(snapshotProvider());
147	    },
148	
149	    listFieldValues(query) {
150	      return selectReceiveFieldValues(snapshotProvider(), query);
151	    },
152	
153	    listRecentInputs(query) {
154	      return selectReceiveRecentInputs(snapshotProvider(), query);
155	    },
156	
157	    listEvents() {
158	      return selectReceiveEvents(snapshotProvider());
159	    },
160	  };
161	}
162	
163	export function createReceiveService(
164	  options: CreateReceiveServiceOptions = {},
165	): ReceiveService {
166	  const state = options.state ?? createReceiveState();
167	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
168	  const frameReader = options.frameReader ?? createDefaultFrameReader();
169	  const reader = createReceiveReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
170	  let reference: ReceiveFrameReferenceSnapshot = {
171	    frames: [],
172	    referenceVersion: state.getSnapshot().referenceVersion,
173	    refreshedAt: INITIAL_REFERENCE_REFRESHED_AT,
174	  };
175	
176	  function apply(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
177	    const snapshot = state.applyOutcome(outcome);
178	    return serviceOutcome(snapshot, [outcome]);
179	  }
180	
181	  function refresh(frames: readonly ReadonlyFrameAsset[]): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
182	    reference = {
183	      frames: cloneFrameReferences(frames),
184	      referenceVersion: reference.referenceVersion + 1,
185	      refreshedAt: now(),
186	    };
187	    const snapshot = state.setReferenceVersion(reference.referenceVersion);
188	    return serviceOutcome(snapshot, [], reference);
189	  }
190	
191	  function ingest(batch: ReceiveInputBatch): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
192	    if (
193	      batch.referenceVersion !== undefined &&
194	      batch.referenceVersion !== reference.referenceVersion
195	    ) {
196	      return apply(
197	        createStaleReceiveInputOutcome(
198	          batch.id,
199	          batch,
200	          reference.referenceVersion,
201	          now(),
202	        ),
203	      );
204	    }
205	
206	    return apply(
207	      processReceiveBatch({
208	        batch,
209	        reference,
210	        processedAt: now(),
211	      }),
212	    );
213	  }
214	
215	  function recordInputError(error: ReceiveInputError): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
216	    return apply(createReceiveInputErrorOutcome(error, now()));
217	  }
218	
219	  return {
220	    ...reader,
221	
222	    refreshFrameReferences(frames) {
223	      return refresh(frames ?? frameReader.findFrames({ direction: 'receive' }));
224	    },
225	
226	    ingestBatch: ingest,
227	
228	    recordInputError,
229	
230	    async drainInputSource(source) {
231	      const outcomes: ReceiveBatchOutcome[] = [];
232	      for (const event of await source.drainEvents()) {
233	        const serviceCall = event.kind === 'batch' ? ingest(event.batch) : recordInputError(event.error);
234	        outcomes.push(...serviceCall.outcomes);
235	      }
236	
237	      return serviceOutcome(state.getSnapshot(), outcomes);
238	    },
239	
240	    reset(scope = 'all') {
241	      const snapshot = state.reset(scope);
242	      if (scope === 'all') {
243	        reference = {
244	          frames: [],
245	          referenceVersion: 0,
246	          refreshedAt: INITIAL_REFERENCE_REFRESHED_AT,
247	        };
248	      }
249	      return serviceOutcome(snapshot);
250	    },
251	  };
252	}
253

55	function getDefaultFieldValues(frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset): Record<string, SendFieldValue> {
56	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
57	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
58	    if (!field.configurable) continue;
59	    if (field.defaultValue !== undefined) {
60	      const num = Number(field.defaultValue);
61	      values[field.id] = !Number.isNaN(num) && field.defaultValue.trim() !== '' ? num : field.defaultValue;
62	    } else if (field.options.length > 0) {
63	      const defaultOpt = field.options.find(o => o.isDefault);
64	      values[field.id] = defaultOpt ? Number(defaultOpt.value) || defaultOpt.value : Number(field.options[0].value) || field.options[0].value;
65	    }
66	  }
67	  return values;
68	}

56	    value: `${connectedCount.value} / ${connectionSummaries.value.length}`,

85	  { label: '帧定义', description: '管理帧结构与字段', icon: 'view_agenda', to: '/frames', color: 'primary' },

89	];
90	
91	function refreshData(): void {
92	  const summaries = listFrameAssetSummaries(frameService.getSnapshot(), {});
93	  frameCount.value = summaries.length;
94	  fieldCount.value = summaries.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.fieldCount, 0);

98

103	    totalFailed: ts.totalFailed,
104	    totalStepsExecuted: ts.totalStepsExecuted,
105	  };
106	
107	  const ss = sendService.getStatistics();
108	  sendStats.value = {
109	    totalSent: ss.totalSent,
110	    totalErrors: ss.totalErrors,
111	    totalBytesSent: ss.totalBytesSent,
112	  };
113	}
114	
115	const polling = usePolling(refreshData, 2000);
116	
117	function navigateTo(to: string): void {
118	  void router.push(to);
119	}
120	
121	onMounted(() => {
122	  refreshData();
123	  polling.start();
124	});

1	import type {
2	  ReceiveCounterSnapshot,
3	  ReceiveEventSnapshot,
4	  ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot,
5	  ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot,
6	  ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot,
7	  ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot,
8	  ReceiveStateSnapshot,
9	  ReceiveUiSnapshot,
10	} from '../core';
11	import {
12	  cloneCounterSnapshot,
13	  cloneEvent,
14	  cloneFieldValue,
15	  cloneFrameStatistic,
16	  cloneIssue,
17	  cloneMatchedFrame,
18	  cloneRecentInput,
19	  cloneSourceStatistic,
20	  cloneStateSnapshot,
21	  cloneUiSnapshot,
22	} from '../core/clone';
23	
24	export interface ReceiveFrameStatsQuery {
25	  readonly frameId?: string;
26	}
27	
28	export interface ReceiveFieldValueQuery {
29	  readonly frameId?: string;
30	  readonly fieldId?: string;
31	}
32	
33	export interface ReceiveRecentInputQuery {
34	  readonly limit?: number;
35	  readonly sourceId?: string;
36	}
37	
38	export function selectReceiveSnapshot(source: ReceiveStateSnapshot): ReceiveStateSnapshot {
39	  return cloneStateSnapshot(source);
40	}
41	
42	export function selectReceiveCounters(source: ReceiveStateSnapshot): ReceiveCounterSnapshot {
43	  return cloneCounterSnapshot(source.counters);
44	}
45	
46	export function selectReceiveFrameStats(
47	  source: ReceiveStateSnapshot,
48	  query: ReceiveFrameStatsQuery = {},
49	): ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot[] {
50	  return source.frameStats
51	    .filter((item) => (query.frameId ? item.frameId === query.frameId : true))
52	    .map(cloneFrameStatistic);
53	}
54	
55	export function selectReceiveSourceStats(
56	  source: ReceiveStateSnapshot,
57	): ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot[] {
58	  return source.sourceStats.map(cloneSourceStatistic);
59	}
60	
61	export function selectReceiveFieldValues(
62	  source: ReceiveStateSnapshot,
63	  query: ReceiveFieldValueQuery = {},
64	): ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot[] {
65	  return source.fieldValues
66	    .filter((item) => (query.frameId ? item.frameId === query.frameId : true))
67	    .filter((item) => (query.fieldId ? item.fieldId === query.fieldId : true))
68	    .map(cloneFieldValue);
69	}
70	
71	export function selectReceiveRecentInputs(
72	  source: ReceiveStateSnapshot,
73	  query: ReceiveRecentInputQuery = {},
74	): ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot[] {
75	  const filtered = source.recentInputs.filter((item) =>
76	    query.sourceId ? item.sourceId === query.sourceId : true,
77	  );
78	  return filtered.slice(0, query.limit ?? filtered.length).map(cloneRecentInput);
79	}
80	
81	export function selectReceiveEvents(source: ReceiveStateSnapshot): ReceiveEventSnapshot[] {
82	  return source.events.map(cloneEvent);
83	}
84	
85	export function selectReceiveUiSnapshot(source: ReceiveStateSnapshot): ReceiveUiSnapshot {
86	  return cloneUiSnapshot({
87	    lifecycle: source.lifecycle,
88	    referenceVersion: source.referenceVersion,
89	    counters: cloneCounterSnapshot(source.counters),
90	    ...(source.currentFrame ? { currentFrame: cloneMatchedFrame(source.currentFrame) } : {}),
91	    frameStats: source.frameStats.map(cloneFrameStatistic),
92	    fieldValues: source.fieldValues.map(cloneFieldValue),
93	    recentInputs: source.recentInputs.map(cloneRecentInput),
94	    ...(source.lastIssue ? { lastIssue: cloneIssue(source.lastIssue) } : {}),
95	  });
96	}

1	import {
2	  RECEIVE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
3	  type ReceiveBatchOutcome,
4	  type ReceiveCounterSnapshot,
5	  type ReceiveEventSnapshot,
6	  type ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot,
7	  type ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot,
8	  type ReceiveLifecycleStatus,
9	  type ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot,
10	  type ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot,
11	  type ReceiveStateSnapshot,
12	} from '../core';
13	import {
14	  cloneBatchOutcome,
15	  cloneCounterSnapshot,
16	  cloneEvent,
17	  cloneFieldValue,
18	  cloneFrameStatistic,
19	  cloneIssue,
20	  cloneMatchedFrame,
21	  cloneRecentInput,
22	  cloneSourceStatistic,
23	  cloneStateSnapshot,
24	} from '../core/clone';
25	import { createRecentInputHex } from '../core/processor';
26	
27	export type ReceiveResetScope = 'all' | 'runtime' | 'statistics';
28	
29	export interface ReceiveStateInitialValue {
30	  readonly lifecycle?: ReceiveLifecycleStatus;
31	  readonly referenceVersion?: number;
32	  readonly recentInputLimit?: number;
33	  readonly eventLimit?: number;
34	}
35	
36	export interface ReceiveStateContainer {
37	  getSnapshot(): ReceiveStateSnapshot;
38	  setReferenceVersion(referenceVersion: number): ReceiveStateSnapshot;
39	  applyOutcome(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): ReceiveStateSnapshot;
40	  reset(scope?: ReceiveResetScope): ReceiveStateSnapshot;
41	}
42	
43	const EMPTY_COUNTERS: ReceiveCounterSnapshot = {
44	  batchCount: 0,
45	  byteCount: 0,
46	  matchedCount: 0,
47	  unmatchedCount: 0,
48	  configErrorCount: 0,
49	  parseErrorCount: 0,
50	  inputErrorCount: 0,
51	  staleInputCount: 0,
52	};
53	
54	function addCounters(
55	  current: ReceiveCounterSnapshot,
56	  delta: ReceiveBatchOutcome['statsDelta'],
57	): ReceiveCounterSnapshot {
58	  return {
59	    batchCount: current.batchCount + delta.batchCount,
60	    byteCount: current.byteCount + delta.byteCount,
61	    matchedCount: current.matchedCount + delta.matchedCount,
62	    unmatchedCount: current.unmatchedCount + delta.unmatchedCount,
63	    configErrorCount: current.configErrorCount + delta.configErrorCount,
64	    parseErrorCount: current.parseErrorCount + delta.parseErrorCount,
65	    inputErrorCount: current.inputErrorCount + delta.inputErrorCount,
66	    staleInputCount: current.staleInputCount + delta.staleInputCount,
67	  };
68	}
69	
70	function mergeFrameStats(
71	  current: readonly ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot[],
72	  delta: readonly ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot[],
73	): ReceiveFrameStatisticsSnapshot[] {
74	  const merged = new Map(current.map((item) => [item.frameId, cloneFrameStatistic(item)]));
75	
76	  for (const item of delta) {
77	    const existing = merged.get(item.frameId);
78	    merged.set(item.frameId, {
79	      frameId: item.frameId,
80	      frameName: item.frameName,
81	      hitCount: (existing?.hitCount ?? 0) + item.hitCount,
82	      byteCount: (existing?.byteCount ?? 0) + item.byteCount,
83	      ...(item.lastMatchedAt ? { lastMatchedAt: item.lastMatchedAt } : {}),
84	      ...(item.lastSourceId ? { lastSourceId: item.lastSourceId } : {}),
85	    });
86	  }
87	
88	  return Array.from(merged.values());
89	}
90	
91	function mergeSourceStats(
92	  current: readonly ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot[],
93	  delta: readonly ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot[],
94	): ReceiveSourceStatisticsSnapshot[] {
95	  const merged = new Map(current.map((item) => [item.sourceId, cloneSourceStatistic(item)]));
96	
97	  for (const item of delta) {
98	    const existing = merged.get(item.sourceId);
99	    merged.set(item.sourceId, {
100	      sourceId: item.sourceId,
101	      connectionId: item.connectionId,
102	      kind: item.kind,
103	      label: item.label,
104	      batchCount: (existing?.batchCount ?? 0) + item.batchCount,
105	      byteCount: (existing?.byteCount ?? 0) + item.byteCount,
106	      matchedCount: (existing?.matchedCount ?? 0) + item.matchedCount,
107	      unmatchedCount: (existing?.unmatchedCount ?? 0) + item.unmatchedCount,
108	      configErrorCount: (existing?.configErrorCount ?? 0) + item.configErrorCount,
109	      inputErrorCount: (existing?.inputErrorCount ?? 0) + item.inputErrorCount,
110	      staleInputCount: (existing?.staleInputCount ?? 0) + item.staleInputCount,
111	      ...(item.lastInputAt ? { lastInputAt: item.lastInputAt } : {}),
112	    });
113	  }
114	
115	  return Array.from(merged.values());
116	}
117	
118	function fieldKey(field: Pick<ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot, 'frameId' | 'fieldId'>): string {
119	  return `${field.frameId}:${field.fieldId}`;
120	}
121	
122	function mergeFieldValues(
123	  current: readonly ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot[],
124	  outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome,
125	): ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot[] {
126	  if (outcome.kind !== 'matched' || !outcome.input) {
127	    return current.map(cloneFieldValue);
128	  }
129	
130	  const merged = new Map(current.map((item) => [fieldKey(item), cloneFieldValue(item)]));
131	  for (const field of outcome.fields) {
132	    merged.set(fieldKey(field), {
133	      ...field,
134	      updatedAt: outcome.processedAt,
135	      sourceId: outcome.input.source.sourceId,
136	    });
137	  }
138	
139	  return Array.from(merged.values());
140	}
141	
142	function createRecentInput(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): ReceiveRecentInputSnapshot | undefined {
143	  if (!outcome.input) {
144	    return undefined;
145	  }
146	
147	  return {
148	    id: outcome.id,
149	    sourceId: outcome.input.source.sourceId,
150	    sourceLabel: outcome.input.source.label,
151	    kind: outcome.kind,
152	    byteLength: outcome.input.bytes.length,
153	    hex: createRecentInputHex(outcome.input.bytes),
154	    occurredAt: outcome.processedAt,
155	    ...(outcome.matchedFrame ? { matchedFrameId: outcome.matchedFrame.frameId } : {}),
156	    issueCodes: outcome.issues.map((issue) => issue.code),
157	  };
158	}
159	
160	function createEvent(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): ReceiveEventSnapshot {
161	  return {
162	    id: outcome.id,
163	    kind: outcome.kind,
164	    occurredAt: outcome.processedAt,
165	    ...(outcome.input ? { sourceId: outcome.input.source.sourceId } : {}),
166	    ...(outcome.matchedFrame ? { frameId: outcome.matchedFrame.frameId } : {}),
167	    ...(outcome.input ? { byteLength: outcome.input.bytes.length } : {}),
168	    issueCodes: outcome.issues.map((issue) => issue.code),
169	  };
170	}
171	
172	function bounded<T>(items: readonly T[], limit: number): T[] {
173	  return items.slice(0, Math.max(0, limit));
174	}
175	
176	function lifecycleForOutcome(outcome: ReceiveBatchOutcome): ReceiveLifecycleStatus {
177	  return outcome.kind === 'matched' || outcome.kind === 'unmatched' ? 'receiving' : 'error';
178	}
179	
180	export function createReceiveState(
181	  initialValue: ReceiveStateInitialValue = {},
182	): ReceiveStateContainer {
183	  const recentInputLimit = initialValue.recentInputLimit ?? 20;
184	  const eventLimit = initialValue.eventLimit ?? 50;
185	  let snapshot: ReceiveStateSnapshot = {
186	    schemaVersion: RECEIVE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
187	    lifecycle: initialValue.lifecycle ?? 'idle',
188	    referenceVersion: initialValue.referenceVersion ?? 0,
189	    counters: cloneCounterSnapshot(EMPTY_COUNTERS),
190	    frameStats: [],
191	    sourceStats: [],
192	    fieldValues: [],
193	    recentInputs: [],
194	    events: [],
195	  };
196	
197	  function replace(next: ReceiveStateSnapshot): ReceiveStateSnapshot {
198	    snapshot = cloneStateSnapshot(next);
199	    return cloneStateSnapshot(snapshot);
200	  }
201	
202	  return {
203	    getSnapshot() {
204	      return cloneStateSnapshot(snapshot);
205	    },
206	
207	    setReferenceVersion(referenceVersion) {
208	      return replace({
209	        ...snapshot,
210	        lifecycle: 'ready',
211	        referenceVersion,
212	        currentFrame: undefined,
213	        fieldValues: [],
214	        lastIssue: undefined,
215	      });
216	    },
217	
218	    applyOutcome(rawOutcome) {
219	      const applied = cloneBatchOutcome(rawOutcome);
220	      const recentInput = createRecentInput(applied);
221	      const lastIssue = applied.issues.find((issue) => issue.severity === 'error') ?? applied.issues[0];
222	
223	      return replace({
224	        ...snapshot,
225	        lifecycle: lifecycleForOutcome(applied),
226	        counters: addCounters(snapshot.counters, applied.statsDelta),
227	        frameStats: mergeFrameStats(snapshot.frameStats, applied.statsDelta.frameHits),
228	        sourceStats: mergeSourceStats(snapshot.sourceStats, applied.statsDelta.sourceHits),
229	        fieldValues: mergeFieldValues(snapshot.fieldValues, applied),
230	        recentInputs: bounded(
231	          recentInput ? [recentInput, ...snapshot.recentInputs.map(cloneRecentInput)] : snapshot.recentInputs,
232	          recentInputLimit,
233	        ),
234	        events: bounded([createEvent(applied), ...snapshot.events.map(cloneEvent)], eventLimit),
235	        ...(applied.kind === 'matched' && applied.matchedFrame
236	          ? { currentFrame: cloneMatchedFrame(applied.matchedFrame) }
237	          : {}),
238	        ...(lastIssue ? { lastIssue: cloneIssue(lastIssue) } : {}),
239	      });
240	    },
241	
242	    reset(scope = 'all') {
243	      if (scope === 'statistics') {
244	        return replace({
245	          ...snapshot,
246	          counters: cloneCounterSnapshot(EMPTY_COUNTERS),
247	          frameStats: [],
248	          sourceStats: [],
249	          fieldValues: [],
250	          recentInputs: [],
251	          events: [],
252	          currentFrame: undefined,
253	          lastIssue: undefined,
254	        });
255	      }
256	
257	      if (scope === 'runtime') {
258	        return replace({
259	          ...snapshot,
260	          lifecycle: snapshot.referenceVersion > 0 ? 'ready' : 'idle',
261	          fieldValues: [],
262	          recentInputs: [],
263	          events: [],
264	          currentFrame: undefined,
265	          lastIssue: undefined,
266	        });
267	      }
268	
269	      return replace({
270	        schemaVersion: RECEIVE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
271	        lifecycle: 'idle',
272	        referenceVersion: 0,
273	        counters: cloneCounterSnapshot(EMPTY_COUNTERS),
274	        frameStats: [],
275	        sourceStats: [],
276	        fieldValues: [],
277	        recentInputs: [],
278	        events: [],
279	      });
280	    },
281	  };
282	}
283	
```

1	import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';
2	import { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';
3	import { getTransportFacade } from '@/platform';
4	import { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';
5	
6	const rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');
7	
8	export interface BootstrapResult {
9	  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;
10	  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';
11	}
12	
13	export function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): BootstrapResult {
14	  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();
15	  if (transportFacade) {
16	    const adapter = createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade });
17	    const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
18	    return { runtime, mode: 'real' };
19	  }
20	
21	  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime();
22	  return { runtime, mode: 'noOp' };
23	}
24	
25	export function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {
26	  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);
27	  return runtime;
28	}
29	
30	export function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {
31	  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);
32	  if (!runtime) {
33	    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');
34	  }
35	
36	  return runtime;
37	}
38	
```

```
1	---
2	doc_type: quality
3	type: rewrite-frontend-conventions
4	status: draft
5	date: 2026-05-11
6	summary: 前端 UI 规范，讨论/设计/实施时参照。每条规则一句话 + 简短原因 + 一个示例。
7	tags:
8	  - rewrite
9	  - frontend
10	  - conventions
11	  - quasar
12	  - performance
13	---
14	
15	# Rewrite 前端 UI 规范
16	
17	样式 token 规则和 UnoCSS 结构性布局职责划分见 CLAUDE.md "目录与职责"，token 定义见 `rewrite/src/css/tokens/`，本文不重复。代码质量规则见 `rewrite-quality-rules.md`。自检 checklist 见 `rewrite-frontend-checklist.md`。
18	
19	---
20	
21	## 1. Quasar 组件使用
22	
23	### Q1. UI 需求优先从 Quasar 内建方案解决
24	
25	不引入第三方 UI 库（ag-Grid、Lucide、vue-sonner 等）。
26	
27	```typescript
28	// ❌ 第三方 toast
29	import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'; toast.success('ok');
30	// ❌ 第三方图标
31	import { Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';
32	
33	// ✅ Quasar 内建
34	$q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: 'ok' });
35	<q-icon name="o_delete" />
36	```
37	
38	### Q2. Quasar prop 控制视觉行为，不用 CSS 覆盖
39	
40	```vue
41	<!-- ❌ -->
42	<q-input style="padding: 4px; height: 32px;" />
43	<q-btn style="box-shadow: none; background: transparent;" />
44	
45	<!-- ✅ -->
46	<q-input dense />
47	<q-btn flat color="primary" />
48	```
49	
50	### Q3. 颜色使用 Quasar brand prop，不硬编码
51	
52	```vue
53	<!-- ❌ -->
54	<q-btn style="background: #1f6feb; color: white;" />
55	<q-badge style="color: green;">已连接</q-badge>
56	
57	<!-- ✅ -->
58	<q-btn color="primary" />
59	<q-badge color="positive">已连接</q-badge>
60	```
61	
62	### Q4. 确认和反馈走 Quasar plugin
63	
64	删除确认用 `$q.dialog()`，操作反馈用 `$q.notify()`。不用 `window.confirm/alert`。
65	
66	```typescript
67	// ❌
68	if (window.confirm('确定删除？')) doDelete();
69	
70	// ✅
71	$q.dialog({ title: '确认删除', message: '不可恢复', cancel: true }).onOk(doDelete);
72	```
73	
74	### Q5. 删除操作必须二次确认
75	
76	级联删除用项目封装的二次确认组件（需输入关键词）。批量操作必须显示进度。
77	
78	---
79	
80	## 2. 表格
81	
82	### T1. 表格用 QTable，大列表开虚拟滚动
83	
84	行数可能超 100 必须开 `virtual-scroll` + 设 `virtual-scroll-item-size` + 设容器高度。
85	
86	```vue
87	<!-- ❌ 10000 行全渲染 -->
88	<q-table :rows="allData" />
89	
90	<!-- ✅ -->
91	<q-table
92	  :rows="data" virtual-scroll
93	  :virtual-scroll-item-size="48"
94	  :table-style="{ maxHeight: 'calc(100vh - 200px)' }"
95	  :rows-per-page-options="[0]"
96	  row-key="id"
97	/>
98	```
99	
100	### T2. 列数 ≥ 3 或有自定义渲染时，列定义抽到 `columns.ts`
101	
102	列宽策略：文本列自适应、状态列设 min-width、操作列固定宽度。不用百分比宽度。QTable `headerStyle` / `style` 中使用 px 值是 API 要求，不视为硬编码违规。
103	
104	### T3. 大数据集排序和分页走 service 层
105	
106	数据量 > 100 行时排序由 feature service 处理，分页用 QTable server-side 模式 + `@request`。不前端排序分页混合。
107	
108	### T4. 空状态和 loading 必须显式处理
109	
110	```vue
111	<q-table :loading="isLoading">
112	  <template #no-data>
113	    <div class="text-center q-pa-lg text-muted">暂无数据</div>
114	  </template>
115	</q-table>
116	```
117	
118	### T5. 行选中用 QTable selection，选中色走 token
119	
120	```vue
121	<!-- ❌ 在数据对象上加 _selected 字段，硬编码背景色 -->
122	:style="{ background: selectedRows.includes(row) ? '#e8f1ff' : '' }"
123	
124	<!-- ✅ -->
125	<q-table selection="multiple" v-model:selected="selected" />
126	```
127	
128	---
129	
130	## 3. 表单
131	
132	### F1. 表单用 QForm + Quasar 表单组件
133	
134	不混用 HTML 原生表单元素。校验用 QInput `:rules` prop（简单）或 feature service（复杂）。不用第三方校验库。
135	
136	```typescript
137	// ✅ 简单校验
138	<q-input v-model="name" :rules="[val => !!val || '请输入名称']" />
139	
140	// ✅ 复杂校验在 service 层
141	const result = await taskService.validateConfig(config);
142	```
143	
144	### F2. 校验错误用组件 error slot，不用 toast
145	
146	错误信息走 `:error` + `:error-message`，颜色走 `status-danger` token。
147	
148	### F3. 超过 20 个字段必须拆分子组件
149	
150	每个子组件管理自己的响应式数据，隔离 Vue 依赖追踪范围。动态字段超 50 用 QVirtualScroll 承载。QInput 校验用 `lazy-rules="ondemand"`。
```

150	每个子组件管理自己的响应式数据，隔离 Vue 依赖追踪范围。动态字段超 50 用 QVirtualScroll 承载。QInput 校验用 `lazy-rules="ondemand"`。
151	
152	### F4. 提交按钮必须 disable/loading 防重复
153	
154	```vue
155	<q-btn label="提交" :loading="isSubmitting" @click="submit" />
156	```
157	
158	---
159	
160	## 4. 弹窗
161	
162	### D1. 弹窗用 QDialog，双向绑定 + @hide 清理
163	
164	```vue
165	<!-- ❌ 单向绑定，无法关闭 -->
166	<q-dialog :model-value="show">
167	
168	<!-- ✅ -->
169	<q-dialog v-model="show" @hide="resetForm">
170	  <q-card>...</q-card>
171	</q-dialog>
172	```
173	
174	### D2. 弹窗宽度用语义 class，不硬编码
175	
176	定义在 `css/tokens/_size.scss` + `css/layers/_utilities.scss`：
177	
178	| class | min-width | 用途 |
179	|-------|-----------|------|
180	| `rw-dialog-sm` | 400px | 单字段确认、简单选择 |
181	| `rw-dialog-md` | 560px | 表单、配置编辑 |
182	| `rw-dialog-lg` | 720px | 多字段编辑、预览 |
183	| `rw-dialog-xl` | 960px | 复合编辑器（任务编辑器、帧编辑器） |
184	
185	所有 class 带 `max-width: 80vw` 响应式下限。禁止在 `<q-card>` 或 scoped SCSS 中硬编码 `min-width`/`max-width` px 值。
186	
187	```vue
188	<!-- ❌ -->
189	<q-card style="width: 600px; max-width: 80vw;">
190	
191	<!-- ✅ -->
192	<q-card class="rw-dialog-md">
193	```
194	
195	---
196	
197	## 5. 页面布局
198	
199	### L1. 页面内边距用 `p-page` / `p-page-compact` UnoCSS class
200	
201	工具栏与内容区间距用 `gap-4`。结构性布局（间距、flex、grid、display、position、sizing）全部走 UnoCSS utility class，不在 `<style>` 中声明。
202	
203	### L2. 侧边栏用 QDrawer，宽度走 `app-drawer` token（232px）
204	
205	### L3. 桌面不做移动端适配
206	
207	不用 xs/sm 断点、不引入 touch 手势库。响应式仅处理窗口缩小，下限 `breakpoint.page-compact`（720px）。
208	
209	---
210	
211	## 6. 颜色与状态色
212	
213	### C1. 颜色只用 semantic-colors token 或 Quasar brand 色
214	
215	禁止在 style/class/SCSS 中硬编码 hex/rgb/hsl。
216	
217	```scss
218	// ✅ SCSS
219	.error-text { color: rw-color('status-danger'); }
220	// ✅ CSS 变量
221	background: var(--rw-color-surface-selected);
222	// ✅ Quasar
223	<q-badge color="negative">
224	```
225	
226	### C2. 状态色固定映射
227	
228	| 语义 | Token | Quasar color |
229	|------|-------|-------------|
230	| 成功/正常/在线 | `status-success` | `positive` |
231	| 警告/注意 | `status-warning` | `warning` |
232	| 错误/失败/离线 | `status-danger` | `negative` |
233	| 信息/提示 | `status-info` | `info` |
234	| 选中/激活 | `surface-selected` | — |
235	| 禁用/静默 | `text-muted` | — |
236	
237	### C3. 文字颜色分级
238	
239	| 层级 | Token | 用途 |
240	|------|-------|------|
241	| 主要 | `text-primary` | 标题、重要数据 |
242	| 默认 | `text-default` | 正文、表格内容 |
243	| 次要 | `text-secondary` | 描述、辅助信息 |
244	| 弱化 | `text-muted` | 时间戳、placeholder |
245	| 最弱 | `text-subtle` | 禁用态、hint |
246	
247	### C4. Token 消费必须走语义 class，禁止 inline style 消费 token
248	
249	禁止在模板 inline style 中直接使用 color/border/background token。已有语义 class 的 token 必须用 class；同一 token 消费模式出现 2+ 次但尚无 class 时，先提取语义 class 再使用。
250	
251	```vue
252	<!-- ❌ -->
253	<span style="color: var(--rw-color-text-muted)">ID</span>
254	<span style="color: var(--rw-color-text-primary)">{{ value }}</span>
255	<div style="border-bottom: var(--rw-border-width-subtle) solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)">
256	
257	<!-- ✅ -->
258	<span class="rw-text-label">ID</span>
259	<span class="rw-text-value">{{ value }}</span>
260	<div class="rw-divider-b">
261	```
262	
263	以下语义 class 在 `css/layers/_utilities.scss` 中统一定义：
264	
265	**文本色**
266	
267	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
268	|-------|------|------|
269	| `rw-text-label` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-muted)` | 字段标签、小标题 |
270	| `rw-text-value` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-primary)` | 字段值、重要数据 |
271	| `rw-text-desc` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-secondary)` | 描述、辅助文字 |
272	| `rw-text-error` | `color: var(--rw-color-status-danger)` | 错误提示 |
273	| `rw-text-warn` | `color: var(--rw-color-status-warning)` | 警告提示 |
274	
275	**边框**
276	
277	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
278	|-------|------|------|
279	| `rw-divider-b` | `border-bottom: ... solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 区域分隔 |
280	| `rw-divider-t` | `border-top: ... solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 区域分隔 |
281	| `rw-divider-l` | `border-left: ... solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 侧边面板分隔 |
282	
283	**面板 / 弹窗**
284	
285	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
286	|-------|------|------|
287	| `rw-panel-base` | `background: var(--rw-color-surface-base)` | 基础面板背景 |
288	| `rw-dialog-sm/md/lg/xl` | 弹窗宽度 | 见 §4 D2 |
289	
290	不在上表中的 token 消费模式出现 2+ 次时，按同一规则提取并补充。
291	
292	---
293	
294	## 7. 数据展示
295	
296	### V1. 数字格式化
297	
298	整数不带小数，大数加千分位，百分比带 `%`，浮点保留有效精度。格式化函数放 `shared/`。
299

1	---
2	doc_type: reference
3	type: rewrite-frontend-quickref
4	status: active
5	date: 2026-05-14
6	summary: 前端速查卡——写新页面/新组件前花 30 秒扫一遍。含 shared/ 完整 API 索引 + 规范红线 + 新页面检查清单。
7	tags:
8	  - rewrite
9	  - frontend
10	  - quickref
11	---
12	
13	# Rewrite 前端速查卡
14	
15	> 写新页面/新组件前先看。详细规则见 `rewrite-frontend-conventions.md`。
16	> shared/ 所有 API 从 `@/shared` 或 `@/shared/composables` 导入。shared 不 import 任何 feature。
17	
18	## composables — 页面必用
19	
20	| composable | 用途 | 替代什么 |
21	|-----------|------|----------|
22	| `useAsyncAction()` | 异步操作防重 + 自动错误通知 | 手写 operatingIds + try/finally |
23	| `usePolling(fn, ms)` | rAF 轮询 + 自动清理 | 手写 rAF / disposed / 节流 |
24	| `useNotify()` | 统一消息通知 | 散落 `$q.notify()` |
25	| `useStableKeys(prefix?)` | v-for 稳定 key 生成 | index key |
26	
27	```typescript
28	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction()          // execute: 加锁→执行→解锁→失败 notify
29	const { start, stop } = usePolling(() => refresh(), 1000)  // 自动 onUnmounted 清理
30	const notify = useNotify()                                  // success / error / info / warning
31	const { keys, syncKeys } = useStableKeys('step')            // keys.value[i] 始终稳定
32	```
33	
34	## utils/ — 纯工具函数
35	
36	写新代码前先查，避免重复造轮子。
37	
38	| 函数 | 签名 | 用途 |
39	|------|------|------|
40	| `deepClone` | `<T>(value: T) → T` | structuredClone 封装 |
41	| `cloneUnknownValue` | `(value: unknown) → unknown` | 递归拷贝（不依赖 structuredClone） |
42	| `formatElapsed` | `(ms: number) → string` | 毫秒 → "Xms" / "Xs" / "Xm Ys" / "Xh Ym" |
43	| `defaultNow` | `() → string` | ISO 8601 时间戳 |
44	
45	## expression/ — 表达式编译与求值
46	
47	| 函数 | 签名 | 用途 |
48	|------|------|------|
49	| `compileExpression` | `(text, functions?) → CompileResult` | 编译单条表达式 |
50	| `compileConditional` | `(branches, fallback?, functions?) → ConditionalCompileResult` | 编译条件分支 |
51	| `compileGroup` | `(expressions, functions?) → GroupCompileResult` | 编译表达式组（含依赖排序） |
52	| `evaluate` | `(compiled, variables) → EvalResult` | 求值单条表达式 |
53	| `evaluateConditional` | `(compiled, variables) → ConditionalEvalResult` | 求值条件分支 |
54	| `evaluateGroup` | `(group, variables) → GroupEvalResult` | 求值表达式组 |
55	| `defaultMathFunctions` | `FunctionTable` | 内置数学函数（abs/floor/ceil/round/min/max/sqrt/pow/sin/cos/tan/log/exp） |
56	
57	**依赖分析**: `extractDependencies(ast) → Set<string>`, `kahnSort(expressions) → { order } | { cycle }`
58	
59	**核心类型**: `VariableMap = ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>`, `VariableValue = number | string | boolean`, `FunctionTable = ReadonlyMap<string, (...args: number[]) => number>`
60	
61	## condition-operators/ — 条件匹配
62	
63	| 函数 | 签名 | 用途 |
64	|------|------|------|
65	| `compareValues` | `(actual, threshold, operator) → boolean` | 通用值比较 |
66	
67	**运算符**: `eq` `neq` `gt` `lt` `gte` `lte` `contains` `change` `any`
68	
69	**类型**: `ComparisonOperator` — 上述运算符的联合类型
70	
71	## timer/ — 定时器管理
72	
73	| 类 | 用途 |
74	|----|------|
75	| `TimerRegistry` | 分组定时器注册表，全局暂停/恢复 |
76	
77	```typescript
78	const timers = new TimerRegistry()
79	timers.register('tick', () => poll(), 1000, 'polling')
80	timers.pauseAll()
81	timers.resumeAll()
82	timers.clearGroup('polling')
83	```
84	
85	## types/ — 共享类型
86	
87	| 类型 | 用途 |
88	|------|------|
89	| `ReadonlyDeep<T>` | 递归将 T 及嵌套属性设为 readonly |
90	| `ValidationIssue` | `{ severity: 'error' | 'warning', code, path, message }` |
91	| `ValidationResult` | `{ valid, issues: readonly ValidationIssue[] }` |
92	
93	## platform-bridge.ts — 平台能力接口
94	
95	定义 renderer 与桌面平台通信的 typed 接口。**不直接消费**，通过 `rewrite/src/platform` facade 间接使用。
96	
97	- `TransportBridge` — 串口/TCP/UDP 连接、写入、事件
98	- `FileBridge` — 文件读写、对话框
99	- `RewritePlatformBridge` — 组合接口
100	
101	---
102	
103	## 语义 class（不用 inline style 消费 token）
104	
105	```
106	文本：rw-text-label / rw-text-value / rw-text-desc / rw-text-error / rw-text-warn
107	边框：rw-divider-b / rw-divider-t / rw-divider-l
108	面板：rw-panel-base
109	弹窗：rw-dialog-sm / rw-dialog-md / rw-dialog-lg / rw-dialog-xl
110	滚动：rw-dialog-scroll-body
111	```
112	
113	完整表见 conventions §6 C4。
114	
115	## 绝对禁止
116	
117	- `catch {}` / `catch () {}` 空错误吞没（O2）
118	- `style="color: var(--rw-color-...)"` inline token → 用语义 class（C4）
119	- `style="width: Npx"` 硬编码像素 → 用 UnoCSS class 或 rw-dialog-*（C4/D2）
120	- `v-for ... :key="i"` index key → 用稳定 ID（P6）
121	- 组件超 300 行不拆
122	- 选项数组/标签映射写在 setup 内 → 放模块级或常量文件（O4）
123	- 同一逻辑出现 2+ 处不提取（O3）
124	- `any` 类型（D6）
125	
126	## 状态声明顺序（O1）
127	
128	```
129	Service 引用 → 业务数据 → 查询/筛选 → UI 状态 → 派生数据 → 操作
130	```
131	
132	## 新页面检查清单
133	
134	1. composable 用了没？（useAsyncAction / usePolling / useNotify）
135	2. shared/ 已有工具函数查了没？（deepClone / formatElapsed / compareValues）
136	3. 标签/选项提取了没？（模块级常量文件，O3/O4）
137	4. 语义 class 用了没？（grep `style=".*var(--rw-` 和 `style="width: \d`)
138	5. v-for key 稳定吗？（grep `:key="i"` / `:key="idx"`）
139	6. catch 都有处理吗？（grep `catch {}` / `catch () {}`）
140	7. 组件超 300 行吗？该拆吗？
141	8. shallowRef 用对了吗？（>50 元素数组必须用，P2）
142	
```

1	import {
2	  createFrameAssetService,
3	  type FrameAssetReader,
4	  type FrameAssetService,
5	} from '@/features/frame';
6	import {
7	  createSettingsService,
8	  type SettingsService,
9	} from '@/features/settings';
10	import {
11	  createStorageLocalReader,
12	  type StorageLocalReader,
13	} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
14	import {
15	  createConnectionService,
16	  type ConnectionService,
17	  type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
18	} from '@/features/connection';
19	import {
20	  createReceiveService,
21	  type ReceiveService,
22	} from '@/features/receive';
23	import {
24	  createSendService,
25	  type SendService,
26	} from '@/features/send';
27	import {
28	  createTaskService,
29	  type TaskService,
30	} from '@/features/task';
31	import {
32	  createCommandIngressService,
33	  createCommandIngressState,
34	  type CommandIngressService,
35	  type ScoeGlobalConfig,
36	} from '@/features/command-ingress';
37	import { ConnectionBackedSendWriter } from './bridges/connection-backed-writer';
38	import { ConnectionBackedTargetResolver } from './bridges/connection-backed-target-resolver';
39	import { ReceiveEventSourceBridge } from './bridges/receive-event-source-bridge';
40	
41	export interface RewriteWiredFeatures {
42	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
43	  readonly frameService: FrameAssetService;
44	  readonly settingsService: SettingsService;
45	  readonly storageReader: StorageLocalReader;
46	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
47	  readonly receiveService: ReceiveService;
48	  readonly sendService: SendService;
49	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
50	  readonly commandIngressService: CommandIngressService;
51	  readonly receiveEventSourceBridge: ReceiveEventSourceBridge;
52	}
53	
54	export interface WireFeaturesOptions {
55	  readonly connectionAdapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
56	}
57	
58	function createDefaultFrameService(): FrameAssetService {
59	  return createFrameAssetService();
60	}
61	
62	function createDefaultStorageReader(): StorageLocalReader {
63	  return createStorageLocalReader(() => ({
64	    records: [],
65	    historyMaterials: [],
66	    csvMaterials: [],
67	    legacyMaterials: [],
68	  }));
69	}
70	
71	export function wireFeatures(
72	  options: WireFeaturesOptions,
73	): RewriteWiredFeatures {
74	  // L0: no cross-dependencies
75	  const frameService = createDefaultFrameService();
76	  const frameReader = frameService;
77	  const settingsService = createSettingsService();
78	  const storageReader = createDefaultStorageReader();
79	
80	  // L1: needs adapter
81	  const connectionService = createConnectionService({
82	    adapter: options.connectionAdapter,
83	  });
84	
85	  // L2: needs L0 + L1
86	  const receiveService = createReceiveService({ frameReader });
87	
88	  const sendWriter = new ConnectionBackedSendWriter(connectionService);
89	  const targetResolver = new ConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService);
90	  const sendService = createSendService({
91	    frameReader,
92	    targetResolver,
93	    transportWriter: sendWriter,
94	  });
95	
96	  // L3: needs L2
97	  const receiveEventSourceBridge = new ReceiveEventSourceBridge();
98	  const taskService = createTaskService({
99	    sendService,
100	    receiveEventSource: receiveEventSourceBridge,
101	  });
102

1	import {
2	  type FrameAssetReader,
3	} from '@/features/frame';
4	import {
5	  type SettingsOperationResult,
6	  type SettingsResetScope,
7	  type SettingsService,
8	} from '@/features/settings';
9	import {
10	  type StorageLocalReader,
11	} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
12	import {
13	  type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
14	} from '@/features/connection';
15	import { wireFeatures, type RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
16	import { routingTick, type RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';
17	
18	export type { RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';
19	export type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
20	
21	export interface RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot {
22	  readonly frame: {
23	    readonly totalFrames: number;
24	    readonly totalFields: number;
25	    readonly selectedFrameName: string | null;
26	  };
27	  readonly settings: {
28	    readonly autoStartRecording: boolean;
29	    readonly csvDefaultOutputPath: string;
30	    readonly csvSaveIntervalMinutes: number;
31	  };
32	  readonly storage: {
33	    readonly localRecordCount: number;
34	    readonly historyHourCount: number;
35	    readonly csvMaterialCount: number;
36	    readonly legacyMaterialCount: number;
37	    readonly lastIssue: {
38	      readonly code: string;
39	      readonly message: string;
40	    } | null;
41	  };
42	}
43	
44	export interface RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
45	  readonly ok: boolean;
46	  readonly issues: readonly {
47	    readonly severity: string;
48	    readonly code: string;
49	    readonly message: string;
50	  }[];
51	}
52	
53	type FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;
54	type SettingsOverviewPort = Pick<SettingsService, 'getRecordingSettings' | 'reset'>;
55	type StorageOverviewPort = Pick<
56	  StorageLocalReader,
57	  | 'getLastIssue'
58	  | 'listCsvMaterials'
59	  | 'listHistoryHours'
60	  | 'listLegacyMaterials'
61	  | 'listLocalRecords'
62	>;
63	
64	export const ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 100;
65	
66	export interface RewriteRuntimeDependencies {
67	  readonly connectionAdapter?: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
68	  readonly frameReader?: FrameOverviewPort;
69	  readonly settingsService?: SettingsOverviewPort;
70	  readonly storageReader?: StorageOverviewPort;
71	}
72	
73	export interface RewriteRuntime {
74	  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
75	  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
76	  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
77	  routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;
78	  startTickDriver(intervalMs?: number): void;
79	  stopTickDriver(): void;
80	  readonly isTickDriverRunning: boolean;
81	  destroy(): void;
82	}
83	
84	function createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {
85	  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };
86	  return {
87	    connect: async () => accepted,
88	    disconnect: async () => accepted,
89	    write: async () => accepted,
90	    cleanup: async () => accepted,
91	    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,
92	  };
93	}
94	
95	function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
96	  return {
97	    ok: result.ok,
98	    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
99	      severity: issue.severity,
100	      code: issue.code,
101	      message: issue.message,
102	    })),
103	  };
104	}
105	
106	export function createRewriteRuntime(
107	  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
108	): RewriteRuntime {
109	  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();
110	  const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
111	
112	  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;
113	  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;
114	  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;
115	
116	  let destroyed = false;
117	  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
118	
119	  function stopTick(): void {
120	    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {
121	      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);
122	      tickIntervalId = null;
123	    }
124	  }
125	
126	  return {
127	    features: wiredFeatures,
128	
129	    getOverviewSnapshot() {
130	      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
131	      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
132	      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
133	      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();
134	
135	      return {
136	        frame: {
137	          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
138	          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
139	          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
140	        },
141	        settings: {
142	          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
143	          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
144	          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
145	        },
146	        storage: {
147	          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
148	          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
149	          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
150	          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
151	          lastIssue: storageIssue
152	            ? {
153	                code: storageIssue.code,
154	                message: storageIssue.message,
155	              }
156	            : null,
157	        },
158	      };
159	    },
160	
161	    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
162	      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
163	    },
164	
165	    routingTick() {
166	      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);
167	    },
168	
169	    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {
170	      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
171	      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
172	        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
173	      }, intervalMs);
174	    },
175	
176	    stopTickDriver() {
177	      stopTick();
178	    },
179	
180	    get isTickDriverRunning() {
181	      return tickIntervalId !== null;
182	    },
183	
184	    destroy() {
185	      if (destroyed) return;
186	      destroyed = true;
187	      stopTick();
188	      wiredFeatures.commandIngressService.dispose();
189	      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();
190	      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();
191	    },
192	  };
193	}
194	
```

1	import type { DisplayService, DisplayFieldMaterial } from '@/features/display';
2	import type { ReceiveBatchOutcome } from '@/features/receive';
3	
4	export function fanOutToDisplay(
5	  service: DisplayService,
6	  outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[],
7	): number {
8	  const fields: DisplayFieldMaterial[] = [];
9	
10	  for (const outcome of outcomes) {
11	    if (outcome.kind !== 'matched') continue;
12	    if (outcome.fields.length === 0) continue;
13	
14	    for (const f of outcome.fields) {
15	      fields.push({
16	        groupId: f.frameId,
17	        dataItemId: f.fieldId,
18	        fieldName: f.fieldName,
19	        value: f.value,
20	        displayValue: f.displayValue,
21	        updatedAt: outcome.processedAt,
22	      });
23	    }
24	  }
25	
26	  if (fields.length === 0) return 0;
27	
28	  const result = service.ingestSourceMaterial({ fields });
29	  return result.ok ? fields.length : 0;
30	}
31	
```

1	import {
2	  cloneDisplaySnapshot,
3	  createDefaultDisplaySnapshot,
4	  type ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot,
5	  type DisplaySnapshot,
6	} from '../core';
7	
8	export interface DisplayStateInitialValue {
9	  readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot;
10	}
11	
12	export interface DisplayStateContainer {
13	  getSnapshot(): DisplaySnapshot;
14	  replaceSnapshot(snapshot: ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot): DisplaySnapshot;
15	  resetSnapshot(snapshot?: ReadonlyDisplaySnapshot): DisplaySnapshot;
16	}
17	
18	export function createDisplayState(
19	  initialValue: DisplayStateInitialValue = {},
20	): DisplayStateContainer {
21	  let snapshot = initialValue.snapshot
22	    ? cloneDisplaySnapshot(initialValue.snapshot)
23	    : createDefaultDisplaySnapshot();
24	
25	  return {
26	    getSnapshot() {
27	      return cloneDisplaySnapshot(snapshot);
28	    },
29	
30	    replaceSnapshot(nextSnapshot) {
31	      snapshot = cloneDisplaySnapshot(nextSnapshot);
32	      return cloneDisplaySnapshot(snapshot);
33	    },
34	
35	    resetSnapshot(nextSnapshot = createDefaultDisplaySnapshot()) {
36	      snapshot = cloneDisplaySnapshot(nextSnapshot);
37	      return cloneDisplaySnapshot(snapshot);
38	    },
39	  };
40	}
41

31	function readCssPixelToken(name: string): number | undefined {
32	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
33	    return undefined;
34	  }
35	
36	  const rawValue = window.getComputedStyle(window.document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name);
37	  const parsedValue = Number.parseFloat(rawValue);
38	
39	  return Number.isFinite(parsedValue) ? parsedValue : undefined;
40	}

1	import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '@/features/task';
2	import { ConnectionToReceiveInputSource } from './bridges/connection-to-receive';
3	import type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
4	
5	export interface RoutingTickResult {
6	  readonly ok: boolean;
7	  readonly error?: string;
8	  readonly eventsRouted: number;
9	  readonly matchesEmitted: number;
10	}
11	
12	export async function routingTick(
13	  features: RewriteWiredFeatures,
14	): Promise<RoutingTickResult> {
15	  const drainOutcome =
16	    await features.connectionService.drainAdapterEvents();
17	
18	  if (!drainOutcome.ok) {
19	    return {
20	      ok: false,
21	      error: drainOutcome.error?.message ?? 'Connection drain failed',
22	      eventsRouted: 0,
23	      matchesEmitted: 0,
24	    };
25	  }
26	
27	  const dataEvents = drainOutcome.events.filter(
28	    (event) => event.kind === 'data' && event.bytes !== undefined,
29	  );
30	
31	  if (dataEvents.length === 0) {
32	    return { ok: true, eventsRouted: 0, matchesEmitted: 0 };
33	  }
34	
35	  const source = new ConnectionToReceiveInputSource(dataEvents);
36	  const receiveOutcome =
37	    await features.receiveService.drainInputSource(source);
38	
39	  const matchInputs: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];
40	  for (const outcome of receiveOutcome.outcomes) {
41	    if (outcome.kind !== 'matched') continue;
42	    if (!outcome.matchedFrame) continue;
43	
44	    const sourceId = outcome.input?.source.sourceId;
45	    const fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null> = {};
46	    for (const field of outcome.fields) {
47	      fieldValues[field.fieldId] = field.value as number | string | null;
48	    }
49	    matchInputs.push({
50	      frameId: outcome.matchedFrame.frameId,
51	      fieldValues,
52	      ...(sourceId ? { sourceId } : {}),
53	    });
54	  }
55	
56	  if (matchInputs.length > 0) {
57	    features.receiveEventSourceBridge.emit(matchInputs);
58	  }
59	
60	  return {
61	    ok: true,
62	    eventsRouted: dataEvents.length,
63	    matchesEmitted: matchInputs.length,
64	  };
65	}
66	
```

```
290	不在上表中的 token 消费模式出现 2+ 次时，按同一规则提取并补充。
291	
292	---
293	
294	## 7. 数据展示
295	
296	### V1. 数字格式化
297	
298	整数不带小数，大数加千分位，百分比带 `%`，浮点保留有效精度。格式化函数放 `shared/`。
299	
300	```vue
301	<!-- ❌ --> <span>{{ rawValue }}</span>  <!-- 1234567.890000001 -->
302	<!-- ✅ --> <span>{{ formatNumber(rawValue) }}</span>  <!-- 1,234,567.89 -->
303	```
304	
305	### V2. 时间格式化
306	
307	日期时间 `YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss`，仅日期 `YYYY-MM-DD`。相对时间只用于实时日志，不用于历史记录。不直接展示 ISO 字符串或时间戳。
308	
309	### V3. 状态用 QBadge + 状态色，空值显示 `--`
310	
311	不显示 `null` / `undefined` / `NaN`。不裸用文字+颜色替代 badge。
312	
313	状态 badge 组件必须泛型化，不硬编码特定业务状态枚举。通过 `statusMap` 配置映射：
314	
315	```vue
316	<!-- ❌ 硬编码 ConnectionLifecycleStatus -->
317	<StatusBadge :status="conn.lifecycle" />
318	
319	<!-- ✅ 泛型 + 外部映射 -->
320	<StatusBadge :status="conn.lifecycle" :status-map="connectionStatusMap" />
321	<StatusBadge :status="task.state" :status-map="taskStatusMap" />
322	```
323	
324	`statusMap` 类型：`Record<string, { label: string; color: string }>`。各 feature 在自己的 `components/` 或 `types` 中定义对应的映射常量。
325	
326	### V4. 物理量必须标注单位，长文本截断 + QTooltip
327	
328	电压 `12.5 V`，温度 `25.3 ℃`，百分比 `85.3%`（无空格）。超长文本用 CSS 截断，QTooltip 展示全文。
329	
330	---
331	
332	## 8. 性能
333	
334	> 本章规则全部来源于旧系统实际踩坑，是 rewrite 性能底线。
335	
336	### P1. 高频数据必须缓冲后批量刷新
337	
338	串口/网络/SCOE 数据不得逐条触发 Vue 响应式。用 rAF（16ms）或定时器缓冲，每帧批量刷一次。UI 刷新频率与数据源频率解耦。
339	
340	```typescript
341	// ❌ 旧系统：每包数据直接穿透到 store
342	serialPort.on('data', data => store.handleReceivedData(data));
343	
344	// ✅ 缓冲层
345	let pending: Frame[] = [];
346	let rafId: number | null = null;
347	function pushFrame(frame: Frame) {
348	  pending.push(frame);
349	  if (!rafId) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(flush);
350	}
351	function flush() {
352	  displayData.value = [...pending]; pending = [];
353	  rafId = null;
354	}
355	```
356	
357	### P2. 大数组/大对象用 shallowRef，整组替换
358	
359	超 50 元素的数组、嵌套超 2 层的对象用 `shallowRef`。更新时 `arr.value = newArr`，不用 `.push()` / `.splice()`。第三方实例和历史数据用 `markRaw()`。
360	
361	```typescript
362	// ❌
363	const list = reactive<Frame[]>([]); list.push(newFrame);
364	// ✅
365	const list = shallowRef<Frame[]>([]); list.value = [...list.value, newFrame];
366	```
367	
368	### P3. 禁止 deep watch 大对象
369	
370	超 20 属性的对象或超 10 元素的数组禁止 `watch(x, fn, { deep: true })`。改为监听具体 computed、在变更点主动调用。
371	
372	```typescript
373	// ❌
374	watch(() => config, handler, { deep: true });
375	// ✅
376	watch(() => config.groups.length, () => rebuild());
377	// 或在变更点显式调用 rebuild()
378	```
379	
380	### P4. computed 返回对象/数组必须保持引用稳定
381	
382	避免 `.filter()` / `.map()` 直接返回（每次新引用）。Vue 3.4+ 用 computed 的 oldValue 参数比较。
383	
384	推荐模式：表格行数据、筛选结果等派生数组用 `shallowRef` + `watch` 手动更新，不用 `computed` + `.map()`/`.filter()`。
385	
386	```typescript
387	// ❌ 每次 frameList 变化都创建全新数组，触发下游全量 diff
388	const tableRows = computed(() =>
389	  frameList.value.map((f, i) => ({ ...f, _index: i + 1 })),
390	);
391	
392	// ✅ shallowRef + watch，整组替换（与 P2 一致）
393	const tableRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
394	watch(frameList, (list) => {
395	  tableRows.value = list.map((f, i) => ({ ...f, _index: i + 1 }));
396	}, { immediate: true });
397	```
398	
399	少量数据（< 50 条）时 computed 也勉强可用，但后续页面数据量可能增大，统一使用 shallowRef 模式减少认知负担。
400	
401	### P5. 定时器用 requestAnimationFrame，不用 setInterval
402	
403	UI 更新和动画驱动用 rAF。长周期轮询（≥1s）可用 setInterval，但必须 onUnmounted 清理。
404	
405	```typescript
406	// ❌ 旧系统：setInterval(fn, 500) 更新表格
407	// ✅ rAF + 节流
408	let last = 0;
409	function loop(now: number) {
410	  if (now - last >= 500) { updateTable(); last = now; }
411	  rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
412	}
413	```
414	
415	### P6. v-for 必须用稳定唯一 key，不用 index
416	
417	```vue
418	<!-- ❌ --> <div v-for="(item, i) in list" :key="i">
419	<!-- ✅ --> <div v-for="item in list" :key="item.id">
420	```
421	
422	### P7. 仪表盘用 v-memo 限制重渲染范围
423	
424	```vue
425	<ConnectionPanel v-memo="[connectionState]" :state="connectionState" />
426	<FrameMetrics v-memo="[frameSnapshot]" :data="frameSnapshot" />
427	```
428	
429	### P8. 模板中禁止内联 .filter() / .map() / .reduce()
430	
431	提取到 computed。
432	
433	---
434	
435	## 9. CSS 动画
436	
437	### A1. transition 禁用 `all`，只列具体属性
438	
439	```css
440	/* ❌ */ transition: all 0.3s;
441	/* ✅ */ transition: transform 0.3s ease, opacity 0.3s ease;
442	```
443	
444	### A2. 动画只用 transform 和 opacity（composite 属性）
445	
446	禁止动画 width/height/top/left/margin 等 layout 属性。状态指示器动画用 CSS @keyframes，不用 JS setInterval 驱动。will-change 只用于 transform/opacity，优先用 `transform: translateZ(0)` 替代。
447	
448	---
449	
450	## 10. Electron UI
451	
452	### E1. IPC 高频数据必须批量化
453	
454	main 进程缓冲串口/网络数据，16ms 批量发送一次。renderer 侧走 P1 缓冲模式。低频操作可直接 IPC。
455	
456	### E2. 提供硬件加速开关
457	
458	应用设置中提供开关，启动时处理 `gpu-process-crashed` 事件。支持 `--disable-gpu` 参数。
459	
460	### E3. 控制单窗口 DOM 节点 ≤ 3000
461	
462	虚拟滚动、懒渲染、v-if（非 v-show）是主要手段。折叠面板折叠时不渲染内容。
463	
464	---
465	
466	## 11. 页面 Composable 模式
467	
468	页面中以下三种模式不得手写，必须使用对应 composable。
469	
470	### CM1. 异步操作用 `useAsyncAction()`
471	
472	操作锁（operatingIds）、try-finally 清理、错误通知统一由 composable 管理。
473	
474	```typescript
475	// ❌ 手写 operatingIds Set + try-finally
476	const operatingIds = ref<Set<string>>(new Set());
477	async function handleConnect(s: ConnectionSummary) {
478	  operatingIds.value = new Set(operatingIds.value).add(s.connectionId);
479	  try { /* ... */ }
480	  finally { const next = new Set(operatingIds.value); next.delete(s.connectionId); operatingIds.value = next; }
481	}
482	
483	// ✅ composable
484	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
485	async function handleConnect(s: ConnectionSummary) {
486	  await execute(s.connectionId, async () => {
487	    const result = await service.connect(config);
488	    // composable 负责 finally 清理 + 错误时 $q.notify
489	  });
490	}
491	```
492	
493	composable 返回：
494	- `isOperating(id: string): boolean` — 查询操作锁
495	- `execute<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | undefined>` — 带锁执行，自动清理，失败时 `$q.notify({ type: 'negative' })`
496	
497	### CM2. 页面轮询用 `usePolling()`
498	
499	rAF + 节流 + disposed 检查 + onUnmounted 清理统一由 composable 管理。
500	
501	```typescript
502	// ❌ 手写 rAF + disposed + 节流
503	let rafId = 0; let lastPollTime = 0; const disposed = ref(false);
504	function tick(now: number) { /* ... */ rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }
505	onMounted(() => { rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); });
506	onBeforeUnmount(() => { disposed.value = true; if (rafId) cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); });
507	
508	// ✅ composable
509	const { start } = usePolling(() => refreshSummaries(), 500);
510	onMounted(start);
511	```
512	
513	composable 返回：
514	- `start(): void` — 启动轮询
515	- `stop(): void` — 停止轮询
516	- `restart(): void` — 重启轮询
517	- 内部自动 onUnmounted 清理
518	
519	### CM3. 通知用 `useNotify()`
520	
521	封装 `$q.notify` 的成功/失败/警告模式，统一消息格式。
522	
523	```typescript
524	const notify = useNotify();
525	notify.success('连接已创建');
526	notify.error('连接失败', result.error?.message);
527	notify.info('操作已取消');
528	```
529	
530	---
531	
532	## 12. 页面代码组织
533	
534	### O1. 状态声明按用途分组
535	
536	页面 `<script setup>` 中的 ref/computed/shallowRef 按以下顺序声明，组间空一行：
537	
538	```typescript
539	// ===== Service 引用 =====
540	const service = runtime.features.xxxService;
541	
542	// ===== 业务数据 =====
543	const items = ref<readonly Xxx[]>([]);
544	
545	// ===== 查询/筛选 =====
546	const searchText = ref('');
547	const statusFilter = ref<Status | ''>('');
548	
549	// ===== UI 状态 =====
550	const showDialog = ref(false);
551	const selectedIds = ref<string[]>([]);
552	
553	// ===== 派生数据 =====
554	const tableRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
555	
556	// ===== 操作 =====
557	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
558	const notify = useNotify();
559	```
560	
561	### O2. 禁止静默吞错误
562	
563	所有 catch 块必须至少 log（`console.error`/`console.warn`）或 notify。空 catch 体（`catch {}` / `catch () {}`）禁止出现。runtime tick、service 调用、文件 I/O、computed 内部、异步操作一律适用。
564	
565	```typescript
566	// ❌ 空 catch 静默吞错误
567	routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch(() => {});
568	try { buildPreview(); } catch { /* nothing */ }
569	
570	// ✅ 至少记录错误
571	routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
572	try { buildPreview(); } catch (err) { console.error('preview failed', err); }
573	```
574	
575	### O3. 2+ 处出现的同一代码模式必须提取
576	
577	标签映射、选项数组、stable key 管理、数组操作样板、默认值工厂等，出现在 2+ 文件/函数中时，提取到 composable、shared 常量或辅助函数。不允许复制粘贴后微调。
578	
579	### O4. 不变数据定义在模块级
580	
581	选项数组、标签映射、枚举→标签 Record 等不随组件实例变化的数据，定义在 `<script setup>` 外（模块级常量）。setup 每次挂载都会执行，不应在其中重建不变数据。
582	
```
