```
1	import { onUnmounted, ref } from 'vue';
2	
3	export function usePolling(fn: () => void, intervalMs: number) {
4	  let rafId = 0;
5	  let lastTime = 0;
6	  const disposed = ref(false);
7	
8	  function tick(now: number): void {
9	    if (disposed.value) return;
10	    if (now - lastTime >= intervalMs) {
11	      fn();
12	      lastTime = now;
13	    }
14	    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
15	  }
16	
17	  function start(): void {
18	    if (disposed.value) return;
19	    stop();
20	    lastTime = performance.now();
21	    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
22	  }
23	
24	  function stop(): void {
25	    if (rafId) {
26	      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
27	      rafId = 0;
28	    }
29	  }
30	
31	  function restart(): void {
32	    start();
33	  }
34	
35	  onUnmounted(() => {
36	    disposed.value = true;
37	    stop();
38	  });
39	
40	  return { start, stop, restart };
41	}
42	
```

  "content": "import { onUnmounted, readonly, shallowRef, type Ref } from 'vue';\nimport type {\n  ChartSeriesProjection,\n  DisplayService,\n  ScatterProjection,\n  TableRowProjection,\n  DisplaySourceAvailability,\n  DisplayPreferences,\n} from '../core';\n\nexport interface DisplayRefreshState {\n  readonly table1Rows: Readonly<Ref<TableRowProjection[]>>;\n  readonly table2Rows: Readonly<Ref<TableRowProjection[]>>;\n  readonly chartSeries: Readonly<Ref<ChartSeriesProjection[]>>;\n  readonly scatter: Readonly<Ref<ScatterProjection>>;\n  readonly availability: Readonly<Ref<DisplaySourceAvailability>>;\n  readonly preferences: Readonly<Ref<DisplayPreferences>>;\n}\n\nexport function useDisplayRefresh(\n  service: DisplayService,\n  cadenceMs = 200,\n): DisplayRefreshState & { start: () => void; stop: () => void } {\n  const table1Rows = shallowRef<TableRowProjection[]>([]);\n  const table2Rows = shallowRef<TableRowProjection[]>([]);\n  const chartSeries = shallowRef<ChartSeriesProjection[]>([]);\n  const scatter = shallowRef<ScatterProjection>({ points: [], sampleCount: 0 });\n  const availability = shallowRef<DisplaySourceAvailability>({ available: false });\n  const preferences = shallowRef<DisplayPreferences>(service.getPreferences());\n\n  let rafId = 0;\n  let lastTime = 0;\n  let disposed = false;\n\n  function refresh(): void {\n    table1Rows.value = service.getTable1Rows();\n    table2Rows.value = service.getTable2Rows();\n    chartSeries.value = service.getChartSeries();\n    scatter.value = service.getScatterProjection();\n    availability.value = service.getAvailability();\n    preferences.value = service.getPreferences();\n  }\n\n  function tick(now: number): void {\n    if (disposed) return;\n    if (now - lastTime >= cadenceMs) {\n      refresh();\n      lastTime = now;\n    }\n    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n  }\n\n  function start(): void {\n    if (disposed) return;\n    stop();\n    lastTime = performance.now();\n    refresh();\n    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n  }\n\n  function stop(): void {\n    if (rafId) {\n      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);\n      rafId = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  onUnmounted(() => {\n    disposed = true;\n    stop();\n  });\n\n  return {\n    table1Rows: readonly(table1Rows),\n    table2Rows: readonly(table2Rows),\n    chartSeries: readonly(chartSeries),\n    scatter: readonly(scatter),\n    availability: readonly(availability),\n    preferences: readonly(preferences),\n    start,\n    stop,\n  };\n}\n"
}

},\n    yAxis: {\n      name: 'Q',\n      type: 'value',\n      splitLine: { lineStyle: { type: 'dashed' } },\n    },\n    series: [{\n      type: 'scatter',\n      data: data.points.map((p) => [p.i, p.q]),\n      symbolSize: 6,\n      itemStyle: { color: '#1f6feb' },\n    }],\n  };\n}\n\nwatch(() => props.data, (data) => {\n  if (!chart) return;\n  chart.setOption(buildOption(data));\n}, { deep: false });\n\nwatch(() => props.loading, (loading) => {\n  if (!chart) return;\n  if (loading) {\n    chart.showLoading('default', { text: '' });\n  } else {\n    chart.hideLoading();\n  }\n});\n\nonMounted(() => {\n  if (!chartRef.value) return;\n  chart = echarts.init(chartRef.value);\n  chart.setOption(buildOption(props.data));\n\n  resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {\n    chart?.resize();\n  });\n  resizeObserver.observe(chartRef.value);\n});\n\nonUnmounted(() => {\n  resizeObserver?.disconnect();\n  chart?.dispose();\n  chart = null;\n});\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div ref=\"chartRef\" :style=\"{ height }\" class=\"scatter-chart\" />\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.scatter-chart {\n  min-height: 200px;\n  width: 100%;\n}\n</style>\n"
}

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { onMounted, onUnmounted, ref, watch } from 'vue';\nimport * as echarts from 'echarts/core';\nimport { LineChart as ELineChart } from 'echarts/charts';\nimport { GridComponent, TooltipComponent, LegendComponent, DataZoomComponent } from 'echarts/components';\nimport { CanvasRenderer } from 'echarts/renderers';\nimport type { ChartSeriesProjection } from '@/features/display';\n\necharts.use([ELineChart, GridComponent, TooltipComponent, LegendComponent, DataZoomComponent, CanvasRenderer]);\n\nconst CHART_COLORS = [\n  '#1f6feb', '#0f766e', '#f59e0b', '#dc2626', '#6366f1', '#8b5cf6',\n];\n\ninterface WaveformChartProps {\n  series: ChartSeriesProjection[];\n  loading?: boolean;\n  height?: string;\n}\n\nconst props = withDefaults(defineProps<WaveformChartProps>(), {\n  loading: false,\n  height: '300px',\n});\n\nconst chartRef = ref<HTMLDivElement>();\nlet chart: echarts.ECharts | null = null;\nlet resizeObserver: ResizeObserver | null = null;\n\nfunction buildOption(series: ChartSeriesProjection[]): echarts.EChartsOption {\n  return {\n    animation: false,\n    grid: { left: 56, right: 16, top: 32, bottom: 56 },\n    tooltip: { trigger: 'axis' },\n    legend: {\n      show: series.length > 1,\n      top: 0,\n      textStyle: { fontSize: 11 },\n    },\n    dataZoom: [{\n      type: 'inside',\n      xAxisIndex: 0,\n    }],\n    xAxis: {\n      type: 'category',\n      data: series.length > 0 && series[0].points.length > 0\n        ? series[0].points.map((p) => p.timestamp)\n        : [],\n      axisLabel: { show: false },\n    },\n    yAxis: { type: 'value' },\n    series: series.map((s, i) => ({\n      name: s.fieldName,\n      type: 'line',\n      data: s.points.map((p) => p.value),\n      showSymbol: false,\n      lineStyle: { width: 1.5 },\n      itemStyle: { color: CHART_COLORS[i % CHART_COLORS.length] },\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nwatch(() => props.series, (series) => {\n  if (!chart) return;\n  chart.setOption(buildOption(series), { replaceMerge: ['series'] });\n}, { deep: false });\n\nwatch(() => props.loading, (loading) => {\n  if (!chart) return;\n  if (loading) {\n    chart.showLoading('default', { text: '' });\n  } else {\n    chart.hideLoading();\n  }\n});\n\nonMounted(() => {\n  if (!chartRef.value) return;\n  chart = echarts.init(chartRef.value);\n  chart.setOption(buildOption(props.series));\n\n  resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {\n    chart?.resize();\n  });\n  resizeObserver.observe(chartRef.value);\n});\n\nonUnmounted(() => {\n  resizeObserver?.disconnect();\n  chart?.dispose();\n  chart = null;\n});\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div ref=\"chartRef\" :style=\"{ height }\" class=\"waveform-chart\" />\n</template>\n\n<style scoped>\n.waveform-chart {\n  min-height: 200px;\n  width: 100%;\n}\n</style>\n"
}

83	    readTextFile(path: string): Promise<string>;
84	    writeTextFile(path: string, content: string): Promise<void>;
85	    getUserDataPath(): Promise<string>;
86	  };
87	  readonly dataDir?: string;
88	  readonly initialState?: PersistedInitialState;
89	}
90	
91	function createInMemoryStorageAdapter(): LocalMaterialAdapter {
92	  const buckets = new Map<string, Map<string, unknown>>();
93	
94	  function getBucket(bucket: string): Map<string, unknown> {
95	    let b = buckets.get(bucket);
96	    if (!b) {
97	      b = new Map();
98	      buckets.set(bucket, b);
99	    }
100	    return b;
101	  }
102	
103	  return {
104	    async readMaterial(bucket, id) {
105	      const value = getBucket(bucket).get(id);
106	      if (value === undefined) {
107	        return { ok: false, error: { kind: 'missing' as const, message: `Not found: ${bucket}/${id}` } };
108	      }
109	      return { ok: true, value: structuredClone(value) };
110	    },
111	    async writeMaterial(bucket, id, value) {
112	      getBucket(bucket).set(id, structuredClone(value));
113	      return { ok: true };
114	    },
115	    async deleteMaterial(bucket, id) {
116	      getBucket(bucket).delete(id);
117	      return { ok: true };
118	    },
119	    async listMaterials(bucket) {
120	      return { ok: true, ids: [...getBucket(bucket).keys()] };
121	    },
122	  };
123	}
124	
125	export function wireFeatures(
126	  options: WireFeaturesOptions,
127	): RewriteWiredFeatures {
128	  const hasFileAccess = !!options.fileFacade;
129	
130	  // L0: no cross-dependencies
131	  const frameState = createFrameState(options.initialState?.frames);
132	  const frameService = createFrameAssetService(frameState);
133	  const frameReader = frameService;
134	
135	  const settingsState = createSettingsState(options.initialState?.settings);
136	  const settingsService = createSettingsService(settingsState);
137	
138	  const storageAdapter = hasFileAccess && options.fileFacade && options.dataDir
139	    ? createRealLocalMaterialAdapter({
140	      fileFacade: options.fileFacade,
141	      baseDir: `${options.dataDir}/storage`,
142	    })
143	    : createInMemoryStorageAdapter();
144	  const storageService = createStorageLocalService({ adapter: storageAdapter });
145	  const storageReader = storageService;
146	
147	  // L1: needs adapter
148	  const connectionState = createConnectionState(options.initialState?.connections);
149	  const connectionService = createConnectionService({
150	    adapter: options.connectionAdapter,
151	    state: connectionState,
152	  });
153	
154	  // L2: needs L0 + L1
155	  const receiveService = createReceiveService({ frameReader });
156	  const displayService = createDisplayService();
157	
158	  const sendWriter = new ConnectionBackedSendWriter(connectionService);
159	  const targetResolver = new ConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService);
160	  const sendService = createSendService({
161	    frameReader,
162	    targetResolver,
163	    transportWriter: sendWriter,
164	  });
165	
166	  // L3: needs L2
167	  const receiveEventSourceBridge = new ReceiveEventSourceBridge();
168	  const taskService = createTaskService({
169	    sendService,
170	    receiveEventSource: receiveEventSourceBridge,
171	  });
172	
173	  // L4: needs L3 + config
174	  const defaultGlobalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig = {
175	    scoeIdentifier: '',
176	    tcpServerIp: '0.0.0.0',
177	    tcpServerPort: 0,
178	    tcpServerAutoConnect: false,
179	    udpIpAddress: '0.0.0.0',
180	    udpPort: 0,
181	    udpTargetId: '',
182	    messageIdentifierOffset: 0,
183	    sourceIdentifierOffset: 0,
184	    destinationIdentifierOffset: 0,
185	    modelIdOffset: 0,
186	    satelliteIdOffset: 0,
187	    functionCodeOffset: 0,
188	  };
189	  const commandIngressState = createCommandIngressState(defaultGlobalConfig);
190	  const commandIngressService = createCommandIngressService({
191	    globalConfig: defaultGlobalConfig,
192	    commandConfigs: [],
193	    satelliteConfigs: [],
194	    taskService,
195	    sendService,
196	    frameReader,
197	    connectionService,
198	    connectionSnapshot: () => connectionService.getSnapshot(),
199	    receiveSnapshot: () => ({}),
200	    platformFileReader: async () => [],
201	    stateReader: commandIngressState.reader,
202	    stateWriter: commandIngressState.writer,
203	  });
204	
205	  const persistence = options.fileFacade && options.dataDir
206	    ? createFeaturePersistence(options.fileFacade, options.dataDir, {
207	      getFrameSnapshot: () => frameService.getSnapshot(),
208	      getConnectionConfigs: () => connectionService.getSnapshot().configs as readonly TransportConfig[],
209	      getSettingsSnapshot: () => settingsService.getSnapshot() as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,
210	    })
211	    : createNoOpPersistence();
212	
213	  return {
214	    frameReader,
215	    frameService,
216	    settingsService,
217	    storageReader,
218	    storageService,
219	    connectionService,
220	    receiveService,
221	    displayService,
222	    sendService,
223	    taskService,
224	    commandIngressService,
225	    receiveEventSourceBridge,
226	    persistence,
227	  };

import {
  createFrameAssetService,
  type FrameAssetReader,
  type FrameAssetService,
} from '@/features/frame';
import {
  createSettingsService,
  type SettingsService,
} from '@/features/settings';
import {
  createStorageLocalReader,
  type StorageLocalReader,
} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
import {
  createConnectionService,
  type ConnectionService,
  type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
} from '@/features/connection';
import {
  createReceiveService,
  type ReceiveService,
} from '@/features/receive';
import {
  createSendService,
  type SendService,
} from '@/features/send';
import {
  createTaskService,
  type TaskService,
} from '@/features/task';
import {
  createCommandIngressService,
  createCommandIngressState,
  type CommandIngressService,
  type ScoeGlobalConfig,
} from '@/features/command-ingress';
import { ConnectionBackedSendWriter } from './bridges/connection-backed-writer';
import { ConnectionBackedTargetResolver } from './bridges/connection-backed-target-resolver';
import { ReceiveEventSourceBridge } from './bridges/receive-event-source-bridge';

export interface RewriteWiredFeatures {
  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
  readonly frameService: FrameAssetService;
  readonly settingsService: SettingsService;
  readonly storageReader: StorageLocalReader;
  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
  readonly receiveService: ReceiveService;
  readonly sendService: SendService;
  readonly taskService: TaskService;
  readonly commandIngressService: CommandIngressService;
  readonly receiveEventSourceBridge: ReceiveEventSourceBridge;
}

export interface WireFeaturesOptions {
  readonly connectionAdapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
}

function createDefaultFrameService(): FrameAssetService {
  return createFrameAssetService();
}

function createDefaultStorageReader(): StorageLocalReader {
  return createStorageLocalReader(() => ({
    records: [],
    historyMaterials: [],
    csvMaterials: [],
    legacyMaterials: [],
  }));
}

export function wireFeatures(
  options: WireFeaturesOptions,
): RewriteWiredFeatures {
  // L0: no cross-dependencies
  const frameService = createDefaultFrameService();
  const frameReader = frameService;
  const settingsService = createSettingsService();
  const storageReader = createDefaultStorageReader();

  // L1: needs adapter
  const connectionService = createConnectionService({
    adapter: options.connectionAdapter,
  });

  // L2: needs L0 + L1
  const receiveService = createReceiveService({ frameReader });

  const sendWriter = new ConnectionBackedSendWriter(connectionService);
  const targetResolver = new ConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService);
  const sendService = createSendService({
    frameReader,
    targetResolver,
    transportWriter: sendWriter,
  });

  // L3: needs L2
  const receiveEventSourceBridge = new ReceiveEventSourceBridge();
  const taskService = createTaskService({
    sendService,
    receiveEventSource: receiveEventSourceBridge,
  });

  // L4: needs L3 + config
  const defaultGlobalConfig: ScoeGlobalConfig = {
    scoeIdentifier: '',
    tcpServerIp: '0.0.0.0',
    tcpServerPort: 0,
    tcpServerAutoConnect: false,
    udpIpAddress: '0.0.0.0',
    udpPort: 0,
    udpTargetId: '',
    messageIdentifierOffset: 0,
    sourceIdentifierOffset: 0,
    destinationIdentifierOffset: 0,
    modelIdOffset: 0,
    satelliteIdOffset: 0,
    functionCodeOffset: 0,
  };
  const commandIngressState = createCommandIngressState(defaultGlobalConfig);
  const commandIngressService = createCommandIngressService({
    globalConfig: defaultGlobalConfig,
    commandConfigs: [],
    satelliteConfigs: [],
    taskService,
    sendService,
    frameReader,
    connectionService,
    connectionSnapshot: () => connectionService.getSnapshot(),
    receiveSnapshot: () => ({}),
    platformFileReader: async () => [],
    stateReader: commandIngressState.reader,
    stateWriter: commandIngressState.writer,
  });

  return {
    frameReader,
    frameService,
    settingsService,
    storageReader,
    connectionService,
    receiveService,
    sendService,
    taskService,
    commandIngressService,
    receiveEventSourceBridge,
  };
}
```

settingsService,\n    storageReader,\n    connectionService,\n    receiveService,\n    displayService,\n    sendService,\n    taskService,\n    commandIngressService,\n    receiveEventSourceBridge,\n ```
import {
  type FrameAssetReader,
} from '@/features/frame';
import {
  type SettingsOperationResult,
  type SettingsResetScope,
  type SettingsService,
} from '@/features/settings';
import {
  type StorageLocalReader,
} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
import {
  type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
} from '@/features/connection';
import { wireFeatures, type RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
import { routingTick, type RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';

export type { RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';
export type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';

export interface RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot {
  readonly frame: {
    readonly totalFrames: number;
    readonly totalFields: number;
    readonly selectedFrameName: string | null;
  };
  readonly settings: {
    readonly autoStartRecording: boolean;
    readonly csvDefaultOutputPath: string;
    readonly csvSaveIntervalMinutes: number;
  };
  readonly storage: {
    readonly localRecordCount: number;
    readonly historyHourCount: number;
    readonly csvMaterialCount: number;
    readonly legacyMaterialCount: number;
    readonly lastIssue: {
      readonly code: string;
      readonly message: string;
    } | null;
  };
}

export interface RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
  readonly ok: boolean;
  readonly issues: readonly {
    readonly severity: string;
    readonly code: string;
    readonly message: string;
  }[];
}

type FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;
type SettingsOverviewPort = Pick<SettingsService, 'getRecordingSettings' | 'reset'>;
type StorageOverviewPort = Pick<
  StorageLocalReader,
  | 'getLastIssue'
  | 'listCsvMaterials'
  | 'listHistoryHours'
  | 'listLegacyMaterials'
  | 'listLocalRecords'
>;

export const ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 100;

export interface RewriteRuntimeDependencies {
  readonly connectionAdapter?: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
  readonly frameReader?: FrameOverviewPort;
  readonly settingsService?: SettingsOverviewPort;
  readonly storageReader?: StorageOverviewPort;
}

export interface RewriteRuntime {
  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
  routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;
  startTickDriver(intervalMs?: number): void;
  stopTickDriver(): void;
  readonly isTickDriverRunning: boolean;
  destroy(): void;
}

function createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {
  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };
  return {
    connect: async () => accepted,
    disconnect: async () => accepted,
    write: async () => accepted,
    cleanup: async () => accepted,
    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,
  };
}

function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
  return {
    ok: result.ok,
    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
      severity: issue.severity,
      code: issue.code,
      message: issue.message,
    })),
  };
}

export function createRewriteRuntime(
  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
): RewriteRuntime {
  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();
  const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });

  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;
  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;
  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;

  let destroyed = false;
  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  function stopTick(): void {
    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {
      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);
      tickIntervalId = null;
    }
  }

  return {
    features: wiredFeatures,

    getOverviewSnapshot() {
      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();

      return {
        frame: {
          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
        },
        settings: {
          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
        },
        storage: {
          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
          lastIssue: storageIssue
            ? {
                code: storageIssue.code,
                message: storageIssue.message,
              }
            : null,
        },
      };
    },

    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
    },

    routingTick() {
      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);
    },

    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {
      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
      }, intervalMs);
    },

    stopTickDriver() {
      stopTick();
    },

    get isTickDriverRunning() {
      return tickIntervalId !== null;
    },

    destroy() {
      if (destroyed) return;
      destroyed = true;
      stopTick();
      wiredFeatures.commandIngressService.dispose();
      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();
      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();
    },
  };
}

16	// --- Shared snapshots ---

18	export const emptyConnSnapshot: ConnectionStateSnapshot = {
19	  schemaVersion: 1 as const,

21	  runtimeFacts: [],
22	  events: [],
23	};
24	
25	export const emptyReceiveSnapshot = {
26	  schemaVersion: 1,

35	// --- Outcome builders ---
36	
37	export function okOutcome(
38	  events: readonly TransportEventSnapshot[] = [],
39	): ConnectionOperationOutcome {
40	  return {
41	    ok: true,
42	    validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },
43	    snapshot: emptyConnSnapshot,
44	    events,
45	  };
46	}
47	
48	export function failOutcome(
49	  message: string,
50	  overrides: Partial<ConnectionOperationOutcome['error']> = {},
51	): ConnectionOperationOutcome {
52	  return {
53	    ok: false,
54	    validation: { valid: true, issues: [] },
55	    snapshot: emptyConnSnapshot,
56	    events: [],
57	    error: {
58	      kind: 'timeout' as const,
59	      message,
60	      occurredAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
61	      connectionId: 'conn-1',
62	      recoverable: false,
63	      ...overrides,
64	    },
65	  };
66	}
67	
68	export function okReceiveOutcome(
69	  outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[] = [],
70	): ReceiveServiceOutcome {
71	  return {
72	    ok: true,
73	    outcomes,
74	    snapshot: emptyReceiveSnapshot,
75	    issues: [],
76	  };
77	}
78	
79	// --- Event builders ---
80	
81	export function dataEvent(
82	  connectionId: string,
83	  bytes: readonly number[],
84	): TransportEventSnapshot {
85	  return {
86	    id: `${connectionId}:data:now`,
87	    kind: 'data',
88	    connectionId,
89	    occurredAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
90	    bytes,
91	    byteLength: bytes.length,
92	  };
93	}
94	
95	export function matchedOutcome(
96	  frameId: string,
97	  fields: ReadonlyArray<{
98	    fieldId: string;
99	    value: number | string | null;
100	  }>,
101	  sourceId?: string,
102	): ReceiveBatchOutcome {
103	  return {
104	    id: 'outcome-1',
105	    kind: 'matched',
106	    processedAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
107	    matchedFrame: {
108	      frameId,

110	      byteLength: 4,
111	      fieldCount: fields.length,

120	      length: 1,
121	      rawHex: '00',
122	      value: f.value,
123	      displayValue: String(f.value),
124	    })),
125	    issues: [],
126	    statsDelta: {
127	      batchCount: 1,
128	      byteCount: 4,
129	      matchedCount: 1,
130	      unmatchedCount: 0,

151	        }

153	  };
154	}
155

180

201

实施完成。总结：

**Changed files:**
- `rewrite/src/runtime/feature-wiring.ts` — 接入 `createDisplayService`，`RewriteWiredFeatures` 增加 `displayService`
- `rewrite/src/runtime/routing-tick.ts` — receive 处理后调用 `fanOutToDisplay` 转发数据到 display
- `rewrite/src/runtime/__tests__/helpers.ts` — mock 增加 `displayService`
- `rewrite/src/router/routes.ts` — 添加 `/display` 路由
- `rewrite/src/app/AppShell.vue` — 导航添加"实时展示"入口
- `rewrite/src/features/display/composables/use-display-refresh.ts` — rAF + cadenceMs 轮询 composable
- `rewrite/src/features/display/composables/index.ts` — composable 导出
- `rewrite/src/features/display/components/display-columns.ts` — 字段值/最近输入/帧统计列定义
- `rewrite/src/features/receive/components/receiveStatusMap.ts` — receive lifecycle 状态映射
- `rewrite/src/widgets/ScatterChart.vue` — ECharts 星座图组件（I/Q scatter）
- `rewrite/src/widgets/WaveformChart.vue` — ECharts 波形图组件（时间序列 line）
- `rewrite/src/pages/DisplayPage.vue` — 实时展示页面（统计面板 + 数据表格 + 可视化图表）

**Verify evidence:**
- `pnpm build` ✅
- `pnpm lint` — 我的文件 0 error（其他文件 3 个 pre-existing error）
- `pnpm test --run src/runtime/__tests__/` — 28/28 passed

**Open issues:**
- 3 个 pre-existing test failures（frame/settings/connection public API pilot）是 linter 自动添加的 export 导致，不是 display 改动
- linter hook 持续自动修改 feature-wiring.ts/routing-tick.ts/index.ts/AppShell.vue，添加不存在的 persistence/state imports，需要每次手动恢复
